# Accepted head certificate after the 131-chapter freeze

Latest local status: one complete closed principal slab at the million-height endpoint has now passed independent external interval evaluation and mathematical review. All fourteen boundary panels have signed-coordinate margin greater than 3/20 and exact winding zero. A separately reviewed maximum-modulus corollary gives |zeta|>3/20 and |1/zeta|<20/3 throughout this same slab. The accepted region is only [.5032,2]+i[1000000-1/8,1000000]; full principal/family coverage and Lean proofs remain open. The final sections supersede earlier pending high-slab wording only at these scopes.

Latest evaluator status: the fixed eight-center finite-EM batching pilot on heights [1999,2000] completed once and passed full independent consumed-output checks and a separate exact saved-data audit. This supersedes earlier pending small-pilot execution wording. It adds evaluator evidence on an already covered segment, with no new spectral region or production speed claim. Exact scope and pins appear in the final completed-batching entry.

Its [independent synthesis review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/RESUME_SYNTHESIS_131_REVIEW_2026-11-11.md) passed. ## Complete external reciprocal-head evidence

The [height1500 count certificate](../qrh-campaign/agents/mellin/PSI_HEAD_COUNTS_CERTIFICATE_2026-11-11.md) passed [independent review](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/PSI_HEAD_COUNTS_REVIEW_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `0BD2AF981C7F8A201F15597A7EE4C7B9812053C4987231C8EB02E5A6AA8BA491`.

All 472 rational checkpoint contours were completed. They give the full positive-ordinate count `N(1500)=1069`, including off-line zeros and multiplicity, and the exact upper sum

    Q1500 < 833975893977619/500000000000000
          = 1.667951787955238 < 1.668 < 2.

The method uses the entire source zetaF function, an explicitly pole-cancelled finite Euler--Maclaurin expression, reusable Taylor blocks, a separate Cauchy remainder, and the uniform EM error. The shared vertical paths and crossbars have exact rational geometry and common polygon vertices. Strict half-plane witnesses and exact winding provide complete counts, not a list of approximate critical-line zeros.

The full frozen evaluator was replayed on all 18,300 blocks and 18,351 panels. A separate implementation checked every rational path, winding, count and head-sum term. An alternate evaluator with different N/M/K, radius, precision and manually constructed exponential coefficients checked 132 selected blocks and their 183 panels, including every block that had needed subdivision. This alternate check is not a second full numeric evaluation of all blocks. All these checks passed.

## A weaker remaining principal strip suffices

Using `Q1500<1.668` with the reviewed analytic count bound `N(t)<=F(t)+5log(t)+20`, the sufficient remaining principal target can be widened to

    .4975 < Re(rho) < .5025 for every nontrivial zero with |Im(rho)|<=1e6.

The independent exact consequence is

    W35 < 23952656684027/10^12 < 23.953 < 24.

This also lies within the low-strip requirement `[.49,.51]` of the refined psi argument. Both ordinate signs, reflection, actual multiplicities and the endpoint-corrected head integral remain in the proof. The wider strip itself is unproved; neither the counts nor their complete winding certificates locate all zeros on or near the critical line. This accepted refinement is available for the next synthesis; the frozen 131-chapter plan still states the stronger sufficient strip `(.499,.501)` with head target2.

The two large finite spectral targets still lacking complete external evidence are the uniform `.555` family assertion through `Hcert(c)=min(Hp_old(c),1e8/c)` and this principal narrow strip through one million. The height14 exclusion and head1500 data are now externally supported. The entire prime checkpoint dataset through5e9 has also been generated and independently recounted.

## Formal and production work remains

None of the new count or contour certificates is a Lean proof. The global QRH provider still needs local compatibility/type/axiom validation. Formal replay must prove the analytic evaluator, interval arithmetic, Taylor and EM bounds, source-function identity, contour count and multiplicity bridges, and exact finite arithmetic. The final theorem may not acquire any new finite or analytic premise.

Production evaluator research is investigating rigorous Riemann--Siegel or approximate-functional-equation methods and batching, since direct high-height EM evaluation does not automatically scale from these bounded runs. Separate source ledgers are checking the pure-kernel prime replay and the contour/argument-principle interfaces. Their evolving drafts are outside the frozen package and this accepted head result.

The original target, inclusive threshold10^27, retained BinaryGoldbachInput and final major/minor/nonprime budget are unchanged. Completion remains unproved; no new Lean build or canonical source edit occurred.

## Completed generic polygon paper proof

The [expanded crossing proof](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/POLYGON_CROSSING_PROOF_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `21ACEA6753546CB2D22E8BE2949FA02A3D6A030B21960C6A024731514F09E08B`, passed [independent paper-proof review](../qrh-campaign/agents/independent-feasibility/REVIEW_POLYGON_CROSSING_PROOF_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `F75647007C294E13132B984A0F6241B516D5113D789AECA922B4DA265A5E73F0`. The coordinator read both in full. Under the stated whole-panel analytic enclosures, it proves the exact half-open crossing identity, degenerate-edge and ray-endpoint cases, connector cancellation, counterclockwise subdivision, and actual finite analytic-order count. No paper-proof gap remains in this generic implication. Its Lean lemmas, checker semantics and application-specific analytic enclosures remain unproved.

The finite Hurwitz calculations also exposed a source convention that must survive formalization. At s=1, Mathlib's totalized Hurwitz value is attached to subtraction of `1/((s-1)*GammaR(s))`. An evaluator subtracting `1/(s-1)` cannot identify its individual finite-part value with that source value. Nonprincipal combinations are instead identified through entire Hurwitz differences, first in Re(s)>1 and then by analytic continuation. This correction is incorporated in the accepted c5 review; no scalar equality at the pole is assumed.

The detailed complex EM source proof and global7/8 compatibility/reflection plan have subsequently passed their separate paper/source reviews, as recorded below. The top-path count experiment at one million has also passed independent review at its explicit external point-evaluator boundary. No historical zero-count helper is an acceptance input.

The reviewed whole-panel bounds keep actual zeta in the right half-plane along the canonical top path. The review also records the producer's stale low-strip status: the external low-height RH result is already accepted through1500, and the remaining location interval is `1500<|Im rho|<=10^6`.

## Accepted normalized Gamma and principal count derivation

The [principal count formal bridge](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/PRINCIPAL_COUNT_FORMAL_BRIDGE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `0A2F4766FCB77668C5461E8A63B97327EF2E6F595E66F805544A7EFA49A4A028`, passed [independent paper/source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/REVIEW_PRINCIPAL_COUNT_FORMAL_BRIDGE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `A4401782284FFF48DFFBA27BB9DE2EFAF20E6BECB09A4F3737BDD220EA739547`. The coordinator read both complete files and checked the review hash. The exact existing API is `Real.eq_Gamma_of_log_convex`; the producer's extra `BohrMollerup` namespace is corrected by the review without changing the frozen note.

The normalized Laplace construction uses `L(z)=H(z)-H(1)`. Its positive-real second derivative is `sum_(n>=0)1/(x+n)^2`; integrating gives the Gamma recurrence. Bohr-Mollerup uniqueness and analytic continuation identify exp(L) with actual Gamma on the right half-plane. Since H(1) is real, no Stirling constant evaluation is needed for the phase. The reviewer checked integrability, differentiation, coefficient signs, telescoping and branch normalization.

The symmetric xi contour fixes `N(T)=1+theta(T)/pi+S(T)` without calibration to any zero count. Real-axis nonvanishing, pole/trivial-zero removal, counterclockwise orientation and total analytic multiplicity `2N(T)` are explicit. Closed-height passage uses right-local constancy. All twelve listed Lean implementation tasks remain open; acceptance of this derivation does not add an axiom or prove a new Lean theorem.

No interval is inconclusive in these two blocks; this remains isolated bounded arithmetic evidence, with no full sign chain, zero count or enlarged spectral coverage.

## Accepted principal modular theta acceleration

The [principal modular-theta construction](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/PRINCIPAL_THETA_MODULAR_ACCELERATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `C64EDFEB89B0F15B8B225241248BC7A751C385247843CE3AFEBF8FCF0382E84D`, passed [independent source and algebra review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/REVIEW_PRINCIPAL_THETA_MODULAR_ACCELERATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `9C2C4348EC29A5C3B4ACD6221CFE96F011C191395877E3654FC69DD81C7987ED`. The coordinator read both complete reports and checked their hashes.

The actual xi kernel is recovered from the theta value and its first two derivatives at `tau=i*exp(2w)`. The construction tracks all three classical theta functions under translation and inversion, including phases, the specified square-root branch and every derivative term. Retaining the value component while unwinding the derivative jet is essential. Overlapping strict tests, stop when Im(tau)>.4 or invert when Im(tau)<.5 after approximate translation, avoid an undecidable equality test at a reduction boundary. Each inversion increases the imaginary part by a factor greater than100/61. The supplied worst-case bounds are27 inversions at height1000000 and35 at60343058.

The reduced series has a uniform Gaussian tail for derivative orders zero through two. This removes the raw square-root-in-height series cutoff in the paper algorithm. It does not bound total runtime: derivative amplification, translation size, working precision and branch certificates still cost work. Compact-panel use needs effective uniform bounds beyond point reduction. A bounded numerical coefficient pilot and its independent review are now assigned; no high-height xi value or spectral range follows from this paper result.

## Accepted nonprincipal modular theta buckets

The [nonprincipal modular-bucket plan](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/NONPRINCIPAL_THETA_MODULAR_BUCKETS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `2EADB0ACEE2B3C8275D9C8CFA815E4E3E7CC39C3CB0EBA0A86763A4877F74F09`, passed [independent source and mathematical review](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/REVIEW_NONPRINCIPAL_THETA_MODULAR_BUCKETS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `FB6A7BD014A4417CD4D3AD2460C0785EDE3838226AA6F9013248863FB3135E70`. The coordinator read both full reports and verified their hashes and the review script/receipt hashes. The independent review supplies 2,266 exact algebra checks in addition to its source-based analytic argument.

The exact signed bucket is `B_(r,a)(z)=c^a*H_a[r/c,0](i*c*z)` for parity a=0 or1. Modular reduction shares one word across the buckets but transports their rational characteristics and phases separately. The odd S multiplier is `-1/tau`; the source derivative's value contribution cancels only after the complete characteristic expression is differentiated. Each principal square root is bound to its actual intermediate point. The reduced Gaussian tail is uniform, while the final error includes the possibly large common reconstruction factor.

An individual signed bucket is not twice its positive-index bucket. Only after contraction with an actual matching-parity character at c>1 does the zero contribution vanish and the factor one-half recover the required positive theta sum. The alternative closed vector uses all residues modulo2c; deleting nonunits before its S transform is invalid. General modular transformations cannot be applied to an unchanged scalar character theta without these adapters.

Direct summation already fills all residue buckets in one pass. Consequently, one short modular series per unit can cost more at large conductors, and the plan does not assert a universal speedup. The author/reviewer pair now assigned to a four-case conductor5/8 pilot will test the characteristic phases, both parity channels and character reconstruction with independent direct sums. No actual coefficient data, spectral coverage or Lean theorem is accepted from the paper plan alone.

The [family-product feasibility note](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/FAMILY_PRODUCT_CERTIFICATION_FEASIBILITY_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `B701615E4D93727BF7A2C290C41F94EDEB40C0E9FE2BCFAB4A725CB6AC4B8973`, passed [independent mathematical/source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/REVIEW_FAMILY_PRODUCT_CERTIFICATION_FEASIBILITY_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `5A2DD26E445829214DEEA92A6EB0C1527533EC6F1064FCF0F3CA9AE5C0C9929C`. The coordinator read both reports, verified all packet hashes and reproduced the producer's exact finite-algebra receipt. Independent rational-complex determinant and residual-moment checks cover synthetic arrays at conductors 5 and 8; they are not spectral data.

For conductor c>1, a rational group-convolution idempotent e projects onto the actual primitive characters. With entire coefficient vector a whose primitive transforms are the actual L-functions, the augmented convolution matrix for delta-e+e*a has determinant exactly their product. Principal poles are excluded by projection; the separate all-level construction explicitly regularizes its principal pole and does not use unsafe numerical divisor cancellation.

A stronger alternative certificate supplies one candidate b holomorphic on a neighborhood of the entire closed rectangle. The residual r=e-e*a*b has primitive transforms 1-L*bhat. A uniform boundary bound below one for each residual, obtained either from its coefficient l1 norm or from T_k=phi(c)*sum_g |r^{*k}(g)|^2<1, extends by the scalar maximum principle and excludes every primitive zero throughout. The moment itself is not holomorphic; the maximum principle applies to the individual scalar residuals. Unrelated panelwise inverses prove only boundary invertibility and cannot replace this global requirement.

This criterion can eliminate individual image polygons and winding records. It does not establish less arithmetic: construction of a compact global inverse, coefficient errors, convolution costs, shared mesh and smallest-factor conditioning remain open. The existing shared transforms already avoid repeated special-function sums. The selected closed bound beta<=111/200 and conductor-dependent heights are unchanged, including real zeros and inclusive endpoints. A bounded conductor-5 comparison against the existing full rectangle is assigned with independent review; no production inverse or full-family certificate exists yet.

## Accepted first-edge export diagnosis

The [single-edge diagnosis](../qrh-campaign/agents/mellin/MODULAR_ENDPOINT_EXPORT_DIAGNOSIS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `C25E171FBDF9A8689CD7D2ACB99F85828534F6C6286D82A895384343109C4DC3`, passed [independent reproduction and review](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/REVIEW_MODULAR_ENDPOINT_EXPORT_DIAGNOSIS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `0091AAB359ED0C11928FBD4948473DDF8EE5AE659FE11505A2438D6A3E3CA335`. The coordinator read both reports and the diagnostic implementation, verified packet hashes, and independently checked the exact stored midpoint/radius and endpoint identities. The reviewer separately reproduced the frozen first edge and passed 62 bounded checks.

At T=1000000, r=0, n=-1, the internal output midpoint is exactly the input midpoint plus one and its radius remains `134742021*2^-283`. The internal translation therefore contains the whole internal input ball with equality. Exporting the input endpoints at 256 bits widens each side by `133693435*2^-283`; the output export adds no width. The exact input endpoints need 283 mantissa bits, whereas cancellation leaves the output endpoints needing only 245. Re-exporting the same input ball at 283 bits is exact. This establishes the cause for this edge only and preserves the correctness of the original endpoint-check rejection.

Exact center/radius records can retain the internal set without rounding its endpoints. Their decoder and operation semantics still require proof; reconstructing through an external ball constructor is not automatically lossless. Forming single-dyadic endpoints by exponent alignment can itself be expensive on other inputs, so this edge's compact endpoints are not a general allocation bound. The other 13 failed translations, three failed quotients and 24 inconclusive coarse reciprocal tests remain unresolved. A separately versioned first-T/first-S receipt is being tested; no full-graph, analytic or Lean repair is claimed.

## Accepted exact compact reciprocal criterion

The [compact reciprocal criterion](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/COMPACT_RECIPROCAL_RECTANGLE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `53E2BCD126E06BCB280F579352BEB05BCB1C939AE4ACC97661F0525B3486434F`, passed [independent mathematical/source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/independent-feasibility/REVIEW_COMPACT_RECIPROCAL_RECTANGLE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `9E7C5548435232AA0660A568F0C6DEF5C0CB9F2DFD9184EDCCFAABDF8FE27675`. The coordinator read both reports, verified all six report/code/receipt pins, and reproduced every deterministic producer field read-only. The independent checker uses a different rational grid and its own edge-extrema oracle.

On ordered input/output rectangles with the input excluding the origin, reciprocal containment is equivalent to four quadratic residuals being nonnegative everywhere. Each residual has the form a*(x^2+y^2)+b*x with b=+1 or -1 after a possible coordinate swap. For a<=0 the four corners suffice. For a>0, choose the inactive coordinate nearest zero and locate the active minimum by endpoint slope signs; the interior-vertex case reduces to 4*a^2*y0^2-1>=0. The checker constructs neither a vertex quotient nor a rational reciprocal candidate.

At most 16 three-term sign checks and 64 dyadic products suffice. The mathematical test is exact, including degenerate intervals, zero output bounds and all quadrants; separate resource rejection is not a mathematical failure. With W-bit supplied mantissas, the reviewed bound is 4W+4 working bits, with a conservative 4W+8 shift policy. Both actual lossless reciprocal gates pass, and rescaling their inputs/outputs by opposite exponents of magnitude 10^100 changes only exponent storage, not the measured mantissa or shift maxima. This is not a Lean timing or a hardened parser result.

The reciprocal routine alone proves no transcendental input. Its inherited receipt still carries the original unproved-seed label, but the separately accepted exact Taylor supplement already supplies that local seed at paper/external scope; this new criterion does not reopen that obligation. Generic compact-arithmetic soundness, typed graph semantics and Lean implementation remain open. No other original lossy-rectangle operation is silently repaired.

## Accepted complete local P-kernel bound

The [one-node P bound](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/PRINCIPAL_ONE_NODE_BOUND_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `5EF2C88A9C98B0CAEE8C8DB277238CAC0EADD1AC00E311D7277F9E563D7C0B14`, passed [independent analytic and exact-arithmetic review](../qrh-campaign/agents/mellin/REVIEW_PRINCIPAL_ONE_NODE_BOUND_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `6ABD86362059F5BFED71A5DA72D59DC6774ED2B338E9E9189899E79C425254B9`. At w=i*(pi/4-1/1000001), the exact seed and lossless graph support the complete word T(-1), S, T(0). The classical theta type is theta3 to theta4 to theta2; the terminal series has no constant term. Its imaginary part is at least 1024. Elementary exponential and geometric bounds control the entire reduced theta2 value and both derivatives by 2^-505, without evaluating a finite theta sum or omitting a tail. All derivative-transport terms, including the value terms, remain in the argument.

The exact amplification estimates give |theta3'|<=2^51*2^-505 and |theta3''|<=2^92*2^-505. The source-normalized P identity then yields |P(w)|<=2^-408<2^-128. This is a positive-radius disk about zero, not an exact-zero assertion; forgetting the disk and later using only coordinate bounds would incur a sqrt(2) norm factor. The result completes one local paper/exact-external calculation, including its analytic input. It is not Lean-checked and supplies no other node, Fourier integral, contour or spectral range.

## Accepted alternative weighted-excess implication

The [weighted spectral-excess proposal](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/WEIGHTED_EXCESS_CERTIFICATE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `5039FA2BE3CD7062E99B75CC82F84F2B154E88714E19F140461FD954F8C67FF2`, passed [independent mathematical and budget review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/REVIEW_WEIGHTED_EXCESS_CERTIFICATE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `D4F0619A50B3DF6BAFFCA051108F64A1DB2491F13BDB9CE31EF17C5F03C0B948`. The coordinator read both reports, verified all six report/code/receipt hashes and independently checked the stored exact final budget inequalities and census. This accepts an alternative conditional implication, not its unproved spectral premise or adoption into the selected strategy.

Keep the uniform beta<=b=111/200 condition through Hcert for c<=100000. For larger active conductors, let Delta_z(beta)=max(z^(beta-1)-z^(b-1),0), with x0=49*10^25 and y0=10^25, and k(beta)=max(x0^(1/8)*Delta_x0(beta),y0^(1/8)*Delta_y0(beta)). The alternative requires K, the sum of k(beta) over all actual low-zero occurrences with analytic multiplicity and both ordinate signs, to be at most 6. Global seven-eighths QRH and all principal obligations are retained. This is weaker than uniform zero exclusion as a condition on spectral data, not a claim that actual zeros violate the old condition.

The decomposition caps only scalar amplitudes. Actual zero locations, Mellin kernels, phases and inverse/conjugate labels remain unchanged. An all-scale estimate is proved for the excess difference itself. Full-strip Gram bounds apply to the capped actual-kernel combination. Exact Gauss support and at-most-two supported induction moduli for c>100000 yield diagonal excess bounds and all five mixed perturbations. A separate pointwise argument covers base gap, including possible reflected partners below .445; averages are not substituted for that consumer.

At K<=6 the sufficient bounds include Aplus<5.156e-9, Astar+Cross<9.388e-7<9.5e-7, N<.044353<1.23 and base gap<.044919<.04712. The optional old Astar<=2.5e-7 allocation is not preserved, but the actual selected combined consumer cap is. The major/minor/nonprime constants and final .00009 reserve are unchanged, as are all separate minor and principal/psi branches.

The retained uniform group has 75000 conductors and 1847865075 primitive characters; the weighted group has 75000 and 3695775113. These are not saved evaluations. Product counts or an actual density bound might certify K without character ownership for each zero, but neither is supplied. Existing QRH-strip and Jensen-window upper bounds alone cannot prove K<=6 for an unchecked row. No actual spectral datum, omitted row, runtime saving or new permissible final premise follows. Later optional mild-violation allocation examples are outside this frozen review and are not adopted here.

## Accepted limits of the literal global inverse implementation

The [global inverse scaling analysis](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/GLOBAL_INVERSE_SCALING_LIMITS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `DDAA08056E5159790D4E7C58BBF7F028538E1C885CF5E8A1E8D0CA0EF4C6C141`, passed [independent code, arithmetic and source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/REVIEW_GLOBAL_INVERSE_SCALING_LIMITS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `761C72C0F5033212A567A40F099A583F79CA384412A18F365DAA54F75081D390`. The accepted family sums were reused, not re-enumerated.

Copying the prototype's fixed pole and panel spacings gives P=32T+1 poles, J=128T+94 models, a dense fitting matrix with (64T+48)*(32T+2) complex entries, and n*(d+1)*P*J inverse-jet products. At the conductor-4 reference height T=25000001, the matrix alone has 1280000144000003808 entries. These are exact costs of the literal construction, not lower bounds on an optimized method. Across the selected nonprincipal family, its literal coefficient list has 172570444910902 complex slots; streaming changes peak memory and compression may change representation size.

The analytic missing quantities are the inverse coefficient norm W and positive residual slack. The weak .555 zero-location target supplies neither a quantitative distance from its boundary nor those bounds. Increasing precision cannot fix a poor finite approximation space. The sufficient divided Taylor-degree rule requires W>0, with W=0 handled as a constant candidate; the logarithmic moment rule requires 0<eta<1, with eta=0 handled directly. These reviewer qualifications accompany acceptance. The fixed N64/M20 L evaluator also has no uniform production-height error guarantee.

The literal global prototype is therefore not a production design. Local closed-slab inverse candidates, structured rational evaluation and faster L evaluation are possible replacements, each requiring its own proof and cost analysis. No impossibility claim for inverse certificates, no measured production runtime and no new spectral assertion follows.

Different slabs may use different candidates. Exact finite closed coverage then supplies the requested region for every actual character, including seams, overlaps, zero height and inclusive endpoints. No agreement or gluing of inverse functions is required. Principal conductor one needs its separate entire-function identity. Aggregate l1 or moment bounds remain boundary tests for the fixed slab's holomorphic scalar residuals; maximum modulus is never applied to those nonholomorphic norms themselves.

Local dictionaries replace the global product P*J by a sum of P_j*J_j. This removes the forced global-dictionary quadratic factor only under explicit bounds on local sizes, degrees, precision and refinements. Peak fitting storage can fall by streaming slabs, while durable coefficient storage can remain linear in total height. High-height L evaluation, actual candidate construction, residual slack and full-family data remain open. The ceil mesh formula describes the chosen initial subdivision, not arbitrary extra subdivisions.

The [nonprincipal reuse audit](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/RATIONAL_PRINCIPAL_BACKEND_NONPRINCIPAL_REUSE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `04BC725844FF3AB7B596BFAF65450F07376A06D557B056540C40CEE21A67D097`, passed [independent interface review](../qrh-campaign/agents/independent-feasibility/REVIEW_RATIONAL_PRINCIPAL_BACKEND_NONPRINCIPAL_REUSE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `0EC6E50EFF24B8001152998B2E3470D1326E0B885FA0AE61A4A6433A67D4248D`, only together with its [explicit domain companion](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/RATIONAL_PRINCIPAL_BACKEND_NONPRINCIPAL_REUSE_DOMAINS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `3BA3C7C01445D362A69DFBEDC6058D889F335F0510ECAA30D3EBBC75AE975B65`. The coordinator read all three and verified their hashes. The original omitted positive-q and center/strip/radius hypotheses; the companion makes them explicit without changing the frozen original or its formulas.

Reusable pieces are exact interval arithmetic, elementary enclosures, integer coefficient sums and strict polynomial tests. The classical three-type principal theta engine does not implement generalized rational-characteristic buckets. Nonprincipal work still needs their exact characteristic phases, odd inversion factor, parity-aware character contraction, certified Gauss normalization and separate node-to-panel allocation. The factor one-half enters only after matching-parity contraction. Negative nodes include the root number and different exponential weights; they are not plain conjugates of positive nodes. Normalized character and time transforms must scale their errors along with their values.

Acceptance retains primitive nonprincipal c>1, matching parity, center real part in [111/200,2], |t0|<=T with T>=1, positive strip width and Gaussian lower bounds, |b|+delta<pi/4, h>0, natural K and degree, 0<=rho<1, the spatial cutoff and genuine nonnegative error radii. The conductor/Taylor bound is not unrestricted in the center real part. No principal precision, four-inversion cap, sixteen-mode tail, error allowance or spectral margin transfers automatically. This identifies missing implementation interfaces; it adds no nonprincipal values, full-family coverage or production speed claim.

## Accepted next implementation preflight and analytic simplification

The [next-milestone memo](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/NEXT_AFTER_RATIONAL_PANEL_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `F9C3588E2BFC0A2DA7AF278F645E402C181FF6549E5455DDEDDE2FB4AE32D823`, and [minimal analytic slice](../qrh-campaign/agents/mellin/RATIONAL_DISK_MINIMAL_ANALYTIC_SLICE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `140A7211D82CAE13F7C3315B94F49A407066A6CE983D07435722405903F21B68`, passed [independent research and mathematical review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/REVIEW_NEXT_AFTER_RATIONAL_PANEL_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `10B81EC97C70D984115DA19256FFA07D01402B77A73FF0C984A2207D581B43EB`. The coordinator read all three final notes, verified their pins and independently recovered the deterministic fixture indices from the unchanged full receipt. This accepts a research handoff, not Lean declarations, a proof graph or implementation.

The fixed arithmetic preflight uses nodes k={0,49,53}, covering all three terminal theta types and the first maximum-depth two-S word, plus all seven weight orders at k=-49,0,49. It requires both generic soundness for the admitted typed arithmetic recipes and closed standard-axioms acceptance of these exact concrete records. An untrusted converter must reconstruct missing arithmetic from fixed recipes; the diagnostic JSON cannot be relabeled as a complete operation proof. Valid earlier-operand references are allowed; forward references and cycles are rejected. A semantically valid alternative graph need not match the producer bytes.

Arithmetic containment under explicit seed, elementary-value and terminal-jet hypotheses is an intermediate theorem. Closed acceptance of its arithmetic data is not a closed actual-P bound. Elementary-function and theta soundness must discharge those hypotheses before any actual-function conclusion. The full same-disk xi theorem still needs all 929 nodes, signed weights, complete finite acceptance and every analytic bridge. The three-record preflight is not a substitute for that end-to-end result or either production spectral obligation.

The [acceptance/cost support note](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/RATIONAL_PANEL_LEAN_ACCEPTANCE_COST_PLAN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `17F2FF4D1069DCE24DBBC423547D3D08995AD3CA6AFE9834749AB95C5F0ECCE4`, specifies separate generation, export, elaboration, kernel-checking and memory measurements for a later implementation experiment. No such experiment has run. A fixed witness/recomputation design, actual kernel acceptance and explicit resource cap must precede a useful timing result; neither JSON size nor heterogeneous Python operation counts yields a Lean forecast.

The independently reviewed analytic simplification defines coefficients as integral moments. For ae strongly measurable K with integrable g(v)=norm(K(v))*exp(abs(v)), integral g<=D, 0<=rho<1 and norm(z)<=rho, the exponential-series tail and a finite sum/integral interchange give

`norm(integral K(v)*exp(z*v) - sum_(j=0)^6 z^j*integral K(v)*v^j/j!) <= D*rho^7/(1-rho)`.

This new paper argument preserves the pilot's exact remainder budget while avoiding identification of seven iterated derivatives and a separate Cauchy coefficient theorem. A bound on a totalized integral alone is insufficient; measurability and integrability are explicit. For the actual P kernel, continuity and the integrable envelope A*exp(4*abs(v)-q*exp(2*abs(v))) supply these conditions once formalized, with integral at most A*(1/q+1/q^2)=D. Actual xi Fourier identity, contour shift, theta semantics, quadrature, spatial tails and finite arithmetic remain necessary. General winding is unnecessary for this positive-real disk, but remains a separate requirement if used by later contour certificates. No new final theorem premise is introduced.

The producer reused six previously accepted top finite-g coefficient models through full-box rational re-import and computed seven new models: six bottom and one left. All fourteen initial whole-boundary panels passed without subdivision. The source rectangle argument principle applies to zetaF with c=2+i*(1000000-1/16), R=4 and h=1. The analytic right-edge bound proves the center nonzero, and Q lies strictly in the radius-two ball. ## Accepted finite EM vertical batching reduction

The [shared finite-EM jet plan](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/FINITE_EM_VERTICAL_BATCHING_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `814AD0B3012FDA4FD81B485139257409E81E6EAC8FA44FD6824A7D21F02C8818`, passed [independent mathematical and interface review](../qrh-campaign/agents/independent-feasibility/REVIEW_FINITE_EM_VERTICAL_BATCHING_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `B8EE7250B9E2AAEE8099A7109C08FF34FBD70E880182E99E2B3F30970C49717E`. The coordinator read the complete plan, support notes and three reviews, checked their hashes, independently reconstructed the principal scalar allocation and counts, and inspected both independent exact-check scripts. No logarithms, phases, moment arrays, transforms, function values or new panels were evaluated.

For exact centers s_j=sigma+i*(t0+j*Delta), retain signed Delta and choose a power-of-two L>=4J. A certified rational approximation to L*Delta*log(n)/(2*pi), accurate within 1/8, supplies an integer bin label q_n with error at most 5/8. The residual r_n=J*(Delta*log(n)-2*pi*q_n/L) then has absolute value below 1. This avoids deciding an exact transcendental bin boundary. The full signed q_n defines the residual; only its residue modulo L indexes a bucket.

Group normalized derivative weights and residual powers into finite moments. Each approximate jet is L times the positive normalized DFT, read at -j modulo L, then contracted with powers of the actual j/J. The real-phase integral Taylor remainder is at most |theta|^p/p!, without an exponential loss. Input moment errors sum over bins; normalized FFT arithmetic errors multiply by L. All correlations are allowed. The [transform contract](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/FINITE_EM_VERTICAL_DFT_CONTRACTS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `170C8F976A8AF8B299E4AB737E6924260B3B1D8328FA54C0F2BF7F6E9720F997`, passed [separate source and algebra review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/REVIEW_FINITE_EM_VERTICAL_DFT_CONTRACTS_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `F2DC3A12F4474E8469D45C741E0F1A7FBA5A1CAF61AEF6A1509749FDE8BFD406`. Its dft_apply citation starts at line106 rather than104; the formula and file pin are correct.

The plan includes every term of the same finite g used by the high-slab evaluator. Bernoulli rising factorials and multiplication by s-1 remain varying finite polynomials. The B=N+1 phase has an explicitly bounded recurrence, including step and rounding error. Complete coefficient errors still need the finite-g Taylor tail, actual-point EM error and division by the full s-1 enclosure before any panel test. No transform of the main sum alone is treated as an actual-zeta certificate.

The [corrected scalar support](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/EM_VERTICAL_BATCH_SCALAR_SUPPORT_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `44C53A6F71D9B00368568D35C08D2ED69EC9ECA746AE61E2DDC8A79C5BA2C52F`, passed [independent scalar review](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/REVIEW_EM_VERTICAL_BATCH_SCALAR_SUPPORT_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `4A7FF42F81E202C7842BEF8A0F294D1CB11F3F9812D91ECC282D39DCE32B0126`. For N=333334, K=24, sigma>=.5032 and rho<=1/8, the weighted main-sum coefficient norm is below 8192. With |s_j-1|>3rho, p=32 gives residual quotient error below 2^-100. The explicit conditional transform allocation still fits below that total using the corrected arithmetic factor 16/7. The earlier 128/63 factor is rejected. This is a weighted quotient bound, not a 2^-100 bound on each individual jet, and does not include unrelated correction or final assembly errors.

The h-streamed schedule charges NKp moment contributions, NK(p-1) residual updates, Kp transforms, KpL*log2(L)/2 butterflies and all correction work. For J=1024 or 4096 and p=32, it uses 256000512 moment contributions and 768 transforms; the separate-center coefficient counts would be 8192016384 or 32768065536. These are different work units with additional precision, elementary-function, correction, storage and serialization costs. The finite-sum reduction is O(NKp+KpJ log J) at fixed p and L=O(J), apart from those costs. It supplies no measured speed ratio, full-height complexity bound or Lean timing.

The proposed downward examples have actual ordinates [999872,1000000] or [999488,1000000] on the left edge. No values on the new portions have been computed. Reusing the old EM numerator and finite-g outer bounds requires their original real-part and absolute-height/norm domains. The batching quotient requires |s_j-1|>3rho. The old final 2^-42 allowance additionally requires actual |s-1|>2^19, which these two examples satisfy. Arbitrary continuation downward does not inherit that denominator bound. Coordinate-square insertion must also charge its norm conversion; the reviewed 3/2 allowance still fits the particular old-slab budget.

The scalar author script/receipt pins are `79D62492632A784D58019302F01C310849BB2B4A1D6AB4D782852F1DFCEA204C` and `B68C8861B4F646C4EC43DEC00E5D364C33A1CEDF8C6F0C5FC976E72210F6F26F`. The independent scalar script/receipt pins are `CFF35FB79563130BCB1A2FDBC46A6BF2289E05CA3E0A794A061A54AEA2E924F1` and `0027F86D69A5F3AB15ACD383A5EE9CBF3854B8010DE2E302714B8331E8FBBD1C`, with 42 exact checks and a read-only full scalar replay. The main review's separate script/receipt pins are `3D54B92ED497B150843281F319E9BFBE88FBE1748DDE69AB93D15EB51B8A5624` and `D83B90662066115BAFF7253193D4316EF254740E2B4E047C26EFA03263AF1EBA`, with 4687 exact label/scalar/count checks. These diagnostics do not certify actual logarithmic bins or FFT outputs.

This acceptance closes the paper batching identity and its error composition. Actual moments, certified transforms, whole-panel margins, closed-contour coverage, provider validation and Lean proofs remain open. Research on one long outer contour versus separate closed slabs is pending. Neither final Goldbach target nor the selected spectral strategy changes.

## Accepted principal long outer-contour design

The [long outer-rectangle plan](../qrh-campaign/agents/mellin/PRINCIPAL_LONG_OUTER_RECTANGLE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `5B3F2776C576F9716693EBE0F814D7A5A694BDDAD8637CDE6E7D36CFD5AB59DC`, passed [independent source and mathematical review](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/REVIEW_PRINCIPAL_LONG_OUTER_RECTANGLE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `996608396AF1F83239D8E039A5DFA81A585F3E5EF48FF93E2C4D2233704C2105`. The coordinator read both notes, checked their hashes, read the reused height-2000 and high-slab source reviews, and checked the real-axis evidence and source theorem signatures. No new model, transform, function value, panel or winding was computed. This accepts the conditional contour design, not zero exclusion on the proposed region.

The proposed closed rectangle is Q=[629/1250,2]+i[2000,1000000]. Its six top horizontal blocks and six bottom blocks already have independently checked whole-panel positive-real enclosures for actual zeta. Restrict the first block at each height from [1/2,3/4] to [.5032,3/4]. The height-2000 receipt has SHA256 `477BD15B17326BCE7333346491BA820C98DB2FE2B07DBDB7A0D11278CD2AE252`; its review has SHA256 `C3CA2ABA8EDA4CE9158CB4BD22203DFBEEE0CEC145A54FC8F0D630346B3FC89D`. These are whole-block facts, not merely total counts. The stored finite-g coefficients, both error sources and full denominator division remain part of their meaning. Old endpoints at 1/2 do not supply new clipped-corner vertices automatically.

The entire right edge has Re(zeta)>1/4 analytically and can be one panel. A complete left edge, all compatible shared rational image vertices and an exact outer winding equal to zero remain required. Computational batches only subdivide the left edge. They do not require intermediate horizontal crossbars. Every join must use the same image vertex in both incident strict half-planes, including batch boundaries and the two clipped corners. Boundary nonvanishing or the difference of total counts N(1000000)-N(2000) cannot replace the outer winding test.

The initial length-1/8 mesh has 7984000 left panels and 7984013 total outer edges. A stacked slab protocol with shared horizontal seams would have 47904006 distinct horizontal model blocks; the outer protocol uses twelve. These are geometry and work-item counts, not a runtime ratio or a claim that this mesh passes. Every batch retains its complete moment, transform, correction, model and actual-zeta error ledger. Changing parameters or descending to lower heights requires the corresponding domain and denominator bounds again.

The local logarithmic integral for actual zeta transfers to entire zetaF through zetaF=(s-1)*zeta. The extra 1/(s-1) integral vanishes because the upper-half-plane logarithm is holomorphic near Q. Apply the source entire-function argument principle with c=2+501000i, R=1000000 and h=1. The right-line bound proves the center nonzero, and every point of Q lies at distance at most 499001.4968, strictly below R/2. Actual positive analytic orders cannot cancel, so winding zero plus boundary nonvanishing excludes all closed Q. No large-circle values or simplicity assumption is needed.

The low-height join includes the real axis. The accepted head contour's complete nonzero real bottom on [-.1,1.1] is recorded in the review with SHA256 `0BD2AF981C7F8A201F15597A7EE4C7B9812053C4987231C8EB02E5A6AA8BA491`; gamma=0 is not inferred from conjugation. The accepted sign/count chunks then supply the external finite-RH conclusion through inclusive height 2000. If the new outer certificate succeeds, reflection and conjugation for nontrivial zeros with 0<beta<1 give the requested closed principal strip [.4968,.5032] through inclusive absolute height one million. Auxiliary multiplicity carriers must still have both heights strictly greater than one million.

This route preserves the principal target and removes internal crossbar obligations. It does not establish the left-edge margins, zero winding, the higher conductor-one cutoff, the nonprincipal family, QRH provider validation or any Lean proof. A zero exactly on beta=.5032 would obstruct this strict boundary protocol even though the selected non-strict strip statement permits it. Failure of a panel or nonzero winding must remain an unresolved or failed certificate. A small batching pilot on an already covered domain is being specified separately; no new evaluation has been authorized by this paper acceptance.

## Accepted finite EM character and height composition

The [joint finite-EM adapter](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/FINITE_EM_CHARACTER_HEIGHT_COMPOSITION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `17D6A2E7478E3C03D8B9406C01A5AD93D4277E33B09009B6550D02357DE54BEC`, passed [independent mathematical and source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/mellin/REVIEW_FINITE_EM_CHARACTER_HEIGHT_COMPOSITION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `9E6004112BFBD8F565FBD2A6871B2E9C123751B5F35C9DEEFE631F7997EAAD64`. The coordinator read both complete notes, checked their hashes and independently derived the conductor scaling, removable pole difference and transform normalizations. No coefficient, function value, transform or family packet was computed.

For conductor c>1 and unit representatives a, absorb the source c^(-s) into the finite main bases n=c*nu+a. This leaves integer-base main sums, half endpoints X_a^(-s)/2 with X_a=cN+a, and Bernoulli correction factors c^(2m-1)*X_a^(1-s-2m). Dropping the conductor power would invalidate the remainder and correction formulas. Absorption also accounts for the log(c) convolution in all normalized derivatives; multiplying old unscaled jets by a single scalar would not.

Subtract the same pole term X_1^(1-s)/(c*(s-1)) in every residue coefficient. Its nonprincipal character sum vanishes. The retained pole difference is the entire function

`P_a(s)=-(d_a/c)*X_1^(1-s)*exprel((1-s)*d_a)`, with `d_a=log(X_a)-log(X_1)`.

At s=1 it equals -d_a/c. Together with the finite main sum, half term and all Bernoulli terms, this defines an entire U_a. The full finite approximation is sum_a chi(a)*U_a. It introduces no artificial zero from multiplying L by s-1. The source proof can establish the full nonprincipal identity on Re(s)>1 and continue it, with the EM remainder, to Re(s)>1-2M. It need not identify individual totalized Mathlib Hurwitz values at s=1 with meromorphic finite parts. Principal rows at s=1 remain excluded from this nonprincipal identity.

For v=phi(c), normalized positive transforms on the actual unit group and time axis give a main-sum scale vL, a positive character label and time label -j modulo L. The unit-group transform retains its CRT structure, including noncyclic factors. There is no additional c^(-s), Gauss factor, parity channel or Gaussian quadrature scale. Every original main term is retained. Input uncertainties sum after normalization cancels; normalized tensor arithmetic error multiplies by vL. Corrections varying with residue and height are still separate obligations.

For sigma+2M>1, each residue's actual EM error has the bound

`4*c^(2M-1)*|(s)_(2M)|*X_a^(1-sigma-2M)/(6^(2M)*(sigma+2M-1))`.

Sum these errors over units for a character bound. The integral representation of P_a gives a height-independent modulus bound, but its actual jet evaluation and arithmetic costs remain unproved. Whole-panel use needs new finite-U outer bounds and Taylor tails, or a separate actual-L coefficient argument. Principal finite-g constants and quotient factors do not transfer.

The reviewed time-first and character-first schedules share finite main work across both axes, with explicit NvKp moment contributions, transform counts and per-residue corrections. Their costs include separate exprel jets and endpoint phases. Crucially the EM truncation ratio still depends on |s|/(N+a/c): rewriting the denominator as cN+a does not remove the compensating conductor powers. This yields no free conductor-dependent speedup, uniform precision or practical all-family estimate.

This fills a new paper composition step between the accepted unit-group character transform and finite-EM vertical batching. Formal source identities, exprel/correction enclosures, concrete transform traces, actual closed panels and every selected primitive character through Hcert remain open. The selected uniform spectral requirements and final Goldbach premises are unchanged.

Both programs, concrete interval operations, telemetry and schemas must be frozen and independently reviewed before a separate execution gate. Current work therefore establishes a reviewed experiment specification, with no new function values, spectral coverage, provider validation or Lean proof.

## Accepted entire pole-difference jet enclosure plan

The [pole-difference jet algorithm](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/POLE_DIFFERENCE_JET_ENCLOSURE_PLAN_2026-11-11.md), corrected SHA256 `3F73AF600725DDB51ED622577BF374251B38C408D0D52D4EA06CFCF1BDD60011`, passed [independent mathematical and input-contract review](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/REVIEW_POLE_DIFFERENCE_JET_ENCLOSURE_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `39D7D84D0706FE27A7DEF211684FA1DCA4D9E3544AB1A496AE435DDF4E04F577`. The coordinator read the original derivation, the corrected input clauses, complete review and diagnostic script, checked all artifact hashes and independently derived the normalized jet and moment-tail formulas. No transcendental value, jet array, evaluator or transform was computed.

For X=cN+1, ell=log(X), d=log((cN+a)/X), E=exp((1-s)*ell) and z=(1-s)*d, define phi_k(z)=integral_0^1 u^k*exp(z*u) du. Every normalized derivative of the exact entire correction satisfies

`P_h(s)=-(d/c)*E*(-1)^h*sum_(k=0..h) ell^(h-k)*d^k/((h-k)!*k!)*phi_k(z)`.

The derivative convolution retains both exponential factors, all factorials and the source 1/c. The a=1 correction vanishes identically. At s=1 the moments are exactly 1/(k+1); the zeroth correction is -d/c, not generally zero. The generic formula divides by none of d, z or s-1 and needs no numerical singularity test.

The common Q-term moment polynomial uses z^m/(m!*(m+k+1)). Its real-parameter integral Taylor remainder has norm at most exp(max(0,Re(z)))*|z|^Q/(Q!*(Q+k+1)). Full prefactor and normalized log-power weights propagate this into each jet and into a retained-polynomial error budget. This is not the omitted whole-function Taylor tail. The uniform finite-U allocation above separately supplies that paper bound and the actual EM allowance.

For center real parts in [.555,2], |Im(s)|<=T and T>=1, d<1/N gives |z|<=(T+1)/N. A coarse complete prefactor bound is 9/2. Thus N proportional to T bounds the moment argument, while log(cN), phase certification, requested accuracy and arithmetic costs still matter. Shared finite powers, moment sums and the normalized convolution give a direct arithmetic envelope O(v*J*(K*Q+K^2)), in addition to the main transforms and other corrections. This is not a runtime or bit-complexity forecast.

The corrected effective input contract is material. Analytic identities hold for every complex s, but a terminating arbitrary-accuracy evaluator requires an exact rational point or a supplied effective point-refinement procedure, plus positive rational error budgets. A fixed nonzero-width s-box cannot generally have its true image width removed by increasing arithmetic precision. The s=1 shortcut requires an exact input identity; the generic formula works without deciding equality. The earlier pending pin `390613DE23688F98C4A8C58FE870F8E501B6B425BC53535F5FCD190BD4D9C4DE` is superseded.

The scheme gives elementary real-log series, an elementary complex-exponential enclosure contract, a terminating rational search for Q and finite-arithmetic refinement for the specified point inputs. It charges all log/phase uncertainty, coordinate-to-norm conversions and character-transform scaling. No special-function oracle is required by the mathematical construction. The crude existence algorithm is not claimed practical at arbitrary height.

The independent diagnostic script and receipt pins are `5C65DDA5369A6AEFC3D63D99EBE4B876892A222A69523CD5A2EFB8D076E0B34D` and `CA711BF918B72CB518A4F3E0CECA1D0C768C76BEB95994D0F63BCF6288F31E81`. Their 11907 finite rational/combinatorial checks support the separate universal proof; they evaluate no log, exponential, phi, P, L or model values. This closes the paper correction-jet algorithm, while elementary checker implementation, actual coefficient packets, character/panel acceptance and all Lean proofs remain open. No final theorem premise changes.

It does not prove inclusion in every old square with that width.

The inherited approximation-error allowance below 2^-44 still applies only on the specified vertical panels. Final polynomial and quotient arithmetic must separately meet its 2^-64 gate, or use exact rational interval operations. No narrow image, nonvanishing or spectral conclusion follows from the scalar allowance.

## Accepted rational proof-cost design and first construction

The [proof-cost design](../qrh-campaign/agents/independent-feasibility/FINITE_EM_RATIONAL_PROOF_COST_DESIGN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `19DB61735647D0267954692733C2CF045B977D540A4CF55B5DBDB1145EE82CB8`, passed [independent mathematical and saved-data review](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/REVIEW_FINITE_EM_RATIONAL_PROOF_COST_DESIGN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `022C335200DCD43E6E9639E978F122F5619199B3EBCC2B98F7B997C1328C8D8E`. The coordinator read both full notes and both exact inventory scripts, checked the pins, and independently reconstructed the shared-denominator and phase-reuse arguments. No elementary value or new model was evaluated.

The coordinator selects direct finite-g certification for the first j=7 actual-image proof design. It still requires all sixteen coefficients, the complete last panel and both owned endpoints in their immutable target boxes. The subsequent target remains all 128 coefficients and seventeen images. This choice removes moment and FFT lemmas from the first actual-image theorem; it does not validate the saved batching records. A promised batch-record theorem still requires all its bins, moments and consumed transforms. The choice rests on fewer mathematical interfaces, not a measured runtime advantage or a claim of optimality.

The direct phase-reuse construction has 2,050 baseline/step phase certificates, 7,175 height products, 131,072 main contributions and 8,584 named arithmetic gates. At epsilon=2^-512 its recurrence `e_(j+1)<=(1+2*epsilon)*e_j+3*epsilon`, with e_0<=8193*epsilon, gives phase error below 2^15*epsilon. Complete direct-g coefficient error is below 2^84*epsilon=2^-428. This includes the full correction and previous-jet term and has no time-bin residual tail. Actual containment in the old squares remains to be checked. The batch comparison has 74,112 gates under exact Horner, or 77,184 with rounded affine Horner. These unlike work units supply no verifier-speed ratio. The main/B phase count excludes shared log/pi and batch twiddle work.

Dyadic leaf sharing avoids an unnecessary product of denominators across all 1,024 terms. With b=1025, each fixed-order moment sum shares denominator `2^(b*(1+h+m)+m)*h!*m!`. A uniform sufficient denominator has 40,113 bits; the stated direct main construction needs 15,929 bits. The full fresh-phase correction bound has 17,309 bits. These are sufficient denominator sizes for specified center schedules, not complete bit-operation, numerator, memory or proof-term bounds.

Elementary polynomials remain a separate cost. Before subsequent squarings, a 513-term exponential polynomial at rational denominator d has sufficient common denominator `d^512*512!`. At d=2^1025 that already exceeds 524,800 bits; it is neither a necessary reduced-result size nor an upper bound on all leaf intermediates. Exact squarings can increase it further. A new inner-rounding schedule is under research and is not assumed by this accepted exact-polynomial design.

The compact saved targets occupy 68,025 JSON bytes for the full actual-image target and 10,799 for j=7. These are target encodings, not new certificates or proof-size lower bounds. Author script/receipt pins are `B638E79245CADF189561F81D37393369A5A7D527628410C2462BA58386B35CB9` and `58FBCA32FBC3D472649EA4B3E31C6C9EB864408A83082346789F3E1FCD6B0782`. Independent script/receipt pins are `C882B9648913A03BE83DCE16EC118736B80A650E96C6AB57030F68258B2DDDE6` and `2342162969282436CBA9388DBF3C79E126373B89919E534E774C42A42459161E`, with 484 checks, including regenerated Bernoulli factors and every moment denominator divisibility case. Rational witnesses, exact typed imports, generic soundness and closed kernel acceptance remain open. No Lean/MM0 ratio or production throughput follows.

## Accepted independent conductor-eight pole-jet allocation

The [independent pole-jet construction](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/C8_INDEPENDENT_POLE_JET_ALLOCATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `59C6448E26CDC8AD7DA944017C555B2F3B3F1A4D70E98B4B275585FA5BC46E48`, passed [independent mathematical review](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/REVIEW_C8_INDEPENDENT_POLE_JET_ALLOCATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `B50F5AF842E71467E821F97070D6315F08E5921F29E16D5362561B48591D851E`. The coordinator read both notes and the scalar script, checked the pins, and independently derived the signs, normalization and complete error sums. No function values were computed.

This gives a second mathematical evaluation path for the same entire P correction. At s=1 its h-th normalized coefficient is `((-log X_a)^(h+1)-(-log297)^(h+1))/(8*(h+1)!)`; order 29 requires numerator power 30. At s=1+q, q=+/-i/8, direct endpoint exponential coefficients N_h give `q*P_h+P_(h-1)=N_h/8`. There is no h multiplier. Exact a=1 jets vanish. The construction bypasses the producer's Q32 moment convolution, while preserving the complete finite-U normalization.

The off-center recurrence amplifies absolute errors by `G=(8^30-1)/7<2^88`. Elementary error at most 2^-240 and complete named-gate error at most 2^-224 yield P error below 2^-134. Adding direct heads, half endpoints, all scaled Bernoulli corrections and character sign sums gives complete finite-L coefficient error below 2^-128. These are coefficients of finite L_fin, not derivatives of actual L inferred from a pointwise EM bound. Actual-L disk containment still uses the separate finite-U Taylor and EM estimates.

The allocation requires exact rational arithmetic between gates, or independently established complete aggregate gate bounds. A 256-bit library setting does not establish them. The prospective radius-2^-128 independent balls have enough theoretical room inside the producer's radius-2^-75 balls, but actual full containment must be checked against the immutable centers. No independent Q32 tail is added to this exact-formula path. Existing precision and resource caps remain unchanged.

The exact scalar script and receipt have SHA256 `8393F886D6D8BD9476874E929FD8386D37EA8AF0CA7169D63DD3203A97469A1F` and `3D2617D0CE1FEFE7604D4DB96409C53AC953C8ADE77EE056E4E954736FB87F7B`, with fifteen checks. The reviewer independently derived the formulas and inequalities without executing that script. No coefficient packet, full disk witness, spectral margin or Lean proof follows from this sufficient allocation.

The design relies on the documented successful-query interpretation; retaining a process object does not guarantee that a post-exit query will be available. Host capability remains untested. Live samples and committed-memory counters cannot silently replace the required lifetime peak. A benign capability preflight must precede reliance on this path.

The external elapsed clock starts before process creation and ends at observed termination, including evaluator serialization and shutdown. An asynchronous kill request is not an observed exit. A final evaluator message alone is insufficient: acceptance requires exit zero, final telemetry, single-process accounting, physical output sizes and matching hashes. ## Accepted inner rounding for elementary certificates

The [inner-rounding allocation](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/FINITE_EM_INNER_ROUNDING_ALLOCATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `60B4BC4414A75B90BF036E0CBA2CAB6F12C270CF7DE1F45C767341D872D383E9`, passed [independent mathematical and scalar review](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/REVIEW_FINITE_EM_INNER_ROUNDING_ALLOCATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `E2AFC3467382506F4D68CDB9E7AA0307C911B0F2195032E505C4EC8E69D6401B`. The coordinator read both complete notes and both scalar scripts, checked the pins, and independently derived the Horner error propagation and fixed-input denominator bounds. No elementary witnesses or values were generated.

The new schedule keeps log/pi error below eta=2^-1024, complex primitive error below 2^16*eta and the old canonical grid 2^-1025. It introduces explicitly checked inner gates with norm error at most delta=2^-1040 and coordinate grid 2^-1041. It is therefore compatible with the existing outer allocation; it does not authorize arbitrary rounding elsewhere.

The 513-term exponential polynomial uses `V_512=1` and `V_k=1+z*V_(k+1)/(k+1)`. Its 512 local rounding errors sum to at most exp(R)*delta at argument norm at most R. Four rounded real squarings give `81*tail+283*delta`; the cis calculation retains both its analytic tail and the full pi-reduction uncertainty. The final complex product fits the unchanged elementary interface.

Contractive Horner evaluation gives atanh polynomial error at most 7*delta/4 and each Machin polynomial error at most 29*delta/24. Log assembly must reuse the internal log2 center before canonical output rounding; multiplying an already eta-rounded log2 by ten is not covered. Exact assembly and final rounding give the original log/pi tolerances. All analytic series tails remain included.

For this fixed pilot, an elementary argument has common denominator dividing `625*2^1030`. Resetting each rounded Horner value to grid 2^-1041 bounds the exact pre-gate denominator by 2,090 bits for exponential updates, 1,073 for atanh updates, 1,066 for Machin updates and 2,083 for squares/products. This avoids the old unrounded exponential-polynomial denominator accumulation. The bounds concern specified pre-gate denominators, not every numerator, temporary arithmetic operand, proof term or later moment/correction calculation. They imply no measured runtime or total certificate-size improvement.

Author script/receipt pins are `660A180424D4209E3DC588524C256144E2B9E9A1FD6B39A0913D3FDC0A7DE343` and `DBBB6A94FF1D74EB86347830E65F559B27DC3596B92F74DC676CABE5C3A1CB99`, with 25 checks. Independent script/receipt pins are `B06762ADDC9EC1A547CC60CE47D06543801D3E9AD7EBA369773DB2FA1EA5E5B6` and `5D101EEA32914A900A9F927BAFDF31F4636FCDA36CF702973FE3CC497586673A`, with 2,872 checks. The coordinator adopts this reviewed inner schedule for the proposed rational elementary implementation. Finite gate soundness, actual signed rounding witnesses, immutable-output containment and closed Lean acceptance remain open; no root premise or spectral requirement changes.

## Accepted signed dyadic rounding witness plan

The [signed rounding plan](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/SIGNED_DYADIC_ROUNDING_WITNESS_PLAN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `EFDC61B6EA7E137A917983AAA496413FBC76D2E59B506D5818A31C2E224C3F4B`, passed [independent mathematical and pinned-source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/REVIEW_SIGNED_DYADIC_ROUNDING_WITNESS_PLAN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `F0D115608E1742220E4CAB26EA8DA930D24F2D1D410ED34609EA7B7DC4DABDA5`. The coordinator read both notes, checked their hashes and adopts this rule for the rational implementation preparation. No Lean lemma has been implemented or checked by this acceptance.

For x=p/q with positive q and S=2^g, the unique nearest-grid mantissa with ties toward positive infinity is certified by `(2m-1)q <= 2pS < (2m+1)q`. It is equivalent to `m=floor(pS/q+1/2)`. The signed error is `-1/(2S) < m/S-x <= 1/(2S)`. The rule handles negative half-integers without truncation-toward-zero mistakes; a rounded zero does not assert an exactly zero input. The two coordinate witnesses give a rational L1 norm bound at most 1/S, fitting the existing inner and canonical-output allowances.

Odd-mantissa normalization is exact and separate. Zero has its own canonical pair; a nonzero even signed mantissa is halved only with an exact divisibility witness. Natural absolute value strictly decreases, proving termination for negative mantissas as well. The final finite witness `m=a*2^k`, with odd a and signed exponent k-g, preserves the original grid value without a second rounding error. The pinned Mathlib floor, round, rational-denominator, integer-division and complex-norm APIs support the proposed proof path, but no elaboration or axiom closure is claimed.

The accepted elementary pre-gate denominator bounds are not bounds on the rounding comparisons. For the specified exponential affine gate, denominator bound 2,090 bits and magnitude below 329 imply compared integer magnitudes below 2^3141, and conservative difference/shifted-numerator magnitudes below 2^3142. These are local sufficient bounds, excluding representation overhead, and establish no runtime claim. Other gates and the complete finite-g calculation retain their separate size obligations.

## Accepted full rational j7 operand bound

The [full operand-bound note](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/RATIONAL_J7_FULL_OPERAND_BOUND_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `69B9FA33FF8C90F6691F642B4311FC6870D96E99A1045903B4156CA97B914C20`, passed [independent paper/source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/REVIEW_RATIONAL_J7_FULL_OPERAND_BOUND_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `3F0B0EC8DD19ED4A8D5AC402ACA33ADB1FF3930150E9A191E09600594FF85305`. This binds the existing producer and independent checker, including their intended elementary, finite-g, scalar-validation, image and fixed negative-control arithmetic paths.

Reduced rational denominators stay below 2^20000 and magnitudes below 2^6000. The resulting raw cross-products and sums stay below 2^52001 before Fraction reduction; signed rounding-witness integers stay below 2^27090. These bounds fit the unchanged 65,536-bit guard. The proof classifies actual division calls separately and does not claim closure under arbitrary rational division. The reviewer independently bounded the common denominators without depending on the author's exact bit counts.

The coordinator read the note, review, size-only script and receipt. The script `3DAC91E058BE3EB023BCC4793290CA3FCBC1F68C161737DBADE30BB629404146` and receipt `4C8EF2DB872312EFE3665B2C4EF6CC81922F74ED86A266F9CE8A4DE6A8B49E01` inspect immutable encodings and scalar sizes only. In particular, the saved 192-bit bound includes expanded dyadic numerators, not just mantissas. This closes the paper arithmetic-size obligation for intended inputs. It does not establish runtime, memory, successful numerical containment, executed controls or a Lean proof. Both rational evaluators remain unrun.

## Accepted rational j7 numerical-supervisor design

The [numerical-supervisor design](../qrh-campaign/agents/integration/RATIONAL_J7_NUMERICAL_SUPERVISOR_DESIGN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `E3FF0DD6DB600A42496767254CFE44322F1E92F5CE564EB97DC95150B6E60150`, passed [independent paper/source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/moebius/REVIEW_RATIONAL_J7_NUMERICAL_SUPERVISOR_DESIGN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `DC0A0E113DE9FF002C4710EB15865F7067DBAD2D5C7C902BBE81AA60DF769CA0`. The coordinator read both in full and verified their hashes. This accepts a conditional design, not executable supervision or host capability.

Each numerical phase preserves its worker's 600/900-second, 512 MiB and 16 MiB limits. A supervisor observes the worker's original-handle exit and lifetime peak; an outer controller does the same for the supervisor; the host binds the controller's exit and final record. Observer limits and the controller's narrower self-observed memory scope are explicit. The numerical outer job expects two lifetime processes, supervisor plus one worker, whereas the benign two-fixture test expects three. Extra processes still fail; this difference does not reinterpret V4's counts.

The design accounts for the producer's five output filenames and candidate/completion/stdout agreement, and the independent checker's different single-result protocol. Final serialization and teardown remain inside external limits. Complete phase output accounting spans worker and observer directories; fresh phase namespaces and immutable input bindings isolate producer and checker. Successful producer sealing is required before the independent phase can begin. Candidate status alone cannot discharge an external resource or arithmetic acceptance requirement.

The design's statement that V4 evidence was still under review is historical to its freeze; the accepted saved-result review above now supplies that evidence. Successful capability, a concrete supervisor/controller implementation and its independent review, shared output-budget enforcement, publication ordering and separate runtime decisions remain open. No numerical supervisor code, mathematical evaluator run or Lean proof follows from this design acceptance.

## Accepted shorter P4 consumer proof route

The [P4 source join](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/PRIME_CONSUMER_SOURCE_JOIN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `B5F377CEED926B70B22E2539086D5C423D474A065D3E10F56D93C93108377B61`, passed [independent paper/source review](../qrh-campaign/agents/major-constants/REVIEW_PRIME_CONSUMER_SOURCE_JOIN_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `1C7975F10CB8EE7F4F8D755F9DFC284D439D788C01496D598D9EC7A9500349FB`. The coordinator read both in full, checked their hashes and inspected the independent scalar script and receipt. Its 22 exact rational assertions pass. The script and receipt hashes are `A7EA3B2FEB74105FD2BA4F5B2CC894812BCE996A4D5E3E5EAC8EACE1E0866897` and `431801E6EB1DCC8429CB3C8D902C0A46250DFB0A8ED80675A3162F1005F97861`.

Adopt this shorter route for the unchanged P4 consumer `sum_vonMangoldt_le_a14`: coefficient 1.0064 and threshold 25000000. The unconditional height-floor argument, translated positive mass estimate and global reflected strip yield at most 38 zeros by multiplicity per length-three sign-bin. Harmonic summation gives an unweighted reciprocal moment below 387. The selected narrow principal strip then gives `W35 < 436`, with the weight defined using `max(beta-1/2,0)`. P4 does not separately need the accurate moment below 24 or its numerical head-count inputs.

The existing smoothing allocation with this coarser moment gives relative error below `.00628000628 < .006281 < .0064` uniformly on `[5e9,exp(35)]`; the existing shared-strip tail handles the upper range. The two finite ranges give upper coefficients 1.00030524 and 1.000397565, and the source floor wrapper transfers the integer result with no additive loss. The sieve/chain and analytic endpoints are covered.

This is an accepted conditional paper reduction, not a finished P4 theorem. The actual finite spectral certificates, compatible global QRH provider, translated-mass/digamma argument with true multiplicities, Gaussian adapters, finite nonprime repair, and prime count/log replay still need proof. The accurate `W35 < 24` estimate remains required elsewhere for the common `.01/log x` envelope. The selected global strategy and final theorem statements are unchanged.

## Accepted finite-zero to mixed-residual transfer

## Accepted finite-U to Hardy sign interface

The [Hardy normalization plan](../qrh-campaign/agents/prime-estimates/FINITE_U_HARDY_NORMALIZATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `06C9D3BF785BB183339D8EC64BE0B5AAD05331184C35356F575A8C126ADB724E`, passed [independent review](../qrh-campaign/agents/zero-sum/REVIEW_FINITE_U_HARDY_NORMALIZATION_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `96AE06D3F4D1701B13E0CFD529C0E53A0E9647A3B079DD19D03E66C734064EC1`, without correction. The coordinator contributed the normalized-phase approach, read the full author/reviewer notes and their scalar scripts, inspected the actual completion and growth source, and checked all15 pins in the review. The separate reviewer independently reconstructed the argument.

For alpha=(2a+1)/4, v=t/2, the elementary phase is v*log(c/pi)+(v/2)*log(alpha^2+v^2)+(alpha-1/2)*atan(v/alpha)-v-v/(12*(alpha^2+v^2)). The normalized Binet remainder has norm at most1/(48*alpha^2). Multiplying the actual L-value by the fixed root phase and this elementary unit phase, then taking its real part, gives exactly P=H*cos(delta), where H is the real unit-normalized Hardy function. The cosine is at least17/18 for even characters and1457/1458 for odd characters, at every real height including zero. Therefore P has the exact same signs and zeros. No Gamma magnitude is evaluated and no fixed additive analytic error must be subtracted from the proxy's sign margin.

For desired tau=2^-p, the actual nonprincipal growth bound supplies B=2(c+1)(V+1)+1. Using finite-U precision p+5 and phase/root/rotation tolerances eta=tau/(64B), with complete coordinate-to-norm and product/serialization allowances, yields a proxy error below3*tau/16<tau/4. A sign requires strict exclusion of zero from the resulting interval. Optional shifted phases give arbitrary accuracy for H itself; they are not needed for this sign-only route. The canonical theta in the Turing count term remains unchanged and cannot be replaced by the elementary phase without its own integral error. Conductor one, central multiplicity, inverse-channel kappa, complete counts and saturation retain their separate obligations.

The author scalar script/receipt hashes are `03C27E09FD35195A8765263B0C9BEF63D41FAFE25E90DF2AD2EA425B79D63272` and `DD5C2BC8A872A3308476CF8781582FCC9FC74B6CF96BCFF9C5D1F1E9DB9D7421`. The independent review script, hash `A025F62FB1A4D8A69873C651EE22A6891852F66179178082BF1F4184AF6B81D3`, passed86 checks. The coordinator's separate [read-only checker](review_hardy_proxy_scalars_20261111.py), SHA256 `064E0BAA5267708C0F1E0CE4750876430CB6AA220CBBDD81A4698A1B5FB8509B`, passed67 exact comparisons. None evaluates an analytic function or supplies a sign. The next evaluator work is a sound executable arithmetic/certificate implementation and full data production, not another Gamma-magnitude requirement for sign tests.

This round completes new research and independent reviews and updates the authoritative record. PlattGRH remains in the source; no Lean declarations, builds, spectral values, source edits or frozen-package changes were made. The cubic argument follows actual extraction, dual coefficients, radial-kernel transfer, initial-bound convergence, descendant monomial comparison and finite exponent iteration. The 7*theta loss is retained. The squarefree-ideal norm and full element-row norm are not identified without their comparison theorems. The deleted-mode review proves the finite source-fiber/mass implications without asserting injectivity of the source-to-child projection. The marked zero-mode review evaluates the signed Mobius density before absolute values, leaving only the empty/empty residual pair under disjointness. The Gamma review obtains the ratio bound from a convergent beta integral with positive parameters. Library inspection uses the target Mathlib cache, so it is not a proof of compatibility with the different QRH-pinned version.

## Accepted quadratic columns, retained-source transfer, principal residue and radial envelope

The [quadratic, retained-source, residue and radial frontier](QRH_QUADRATIC_RETAINED_RESIDUE_RADIAL_FRONTIER_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `4C33F3D113D174FDB449DB67A8228CD6D29496D6B2AC85886C52B7FFB6ED971F`, consolidates four further author/reviewer pairs. Its [independent synthesis review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/REVIEW_QRH_QUADRATIC_RETAINED_RESIDUE_RADIAL_FRONTIER_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `5B9652846C017D721B7570A622F704EA8E8228A33E57C40D87A202C354B597CB`, passed without correction. Both records preserve the internal hypotheses and unvalidated lower inputs. All eight report hashes appear in the frontier.

The quadratic product-column argument groups arbitrary coefficients by product and pays one divisor multiplicity through Cauchy. Its four bad-prime sectors cost four, and its reversed-dimension matrix comparison uses the actual conjugate transpose. Products themselves must be squarefree, and K,X>=1. The constant is chosen before those scales and coefficients. The inspected upper sharp-sieve assembly does not by itself validate masked Poisson, the selected scalar majorant, initial estimates or all character/arithmetic foundations.

The retained-source transfer pays the many-to-one physical map by squarefree-divisor fibers. Its coefficient bound Gamma is linear because the energy weight is |w|, not |w|^2. Actual negative height, cross/core coefficients and the32*512 mass factor remain. The global dependent-pair source preserves every outer index and keeps the full sum of second energies. The separate live-key count first partitions all nonzero source terms, then counts occupied eight-coordinate boxes. The resulting small-power budget uses Z>=2 and the stated upper bounds without adding lower bounds on r or ell. Child moment bounds and the actual second-Poisson identity remain separate obligations.

The principal residue follows from the actual finite coordinate map and principal unit indicator. The coefficient sum is the positive product of kernel and unit-group cardinalities, including the singleton quotient. Exact parity, theta and six-unit normalization give positive residue pi*g0/6. The local proof requires neither primitivity nor a zero-free-region premise. Its period-independent formula is a paper consequence, not a new compiled theorem or a uniform positive lower bound over moduli. The underlying theta/Mellin and ideal-series identification remain explicit foundations.

The radial-envelope argument constructs the actual normalized Schwartz transform and supplies its Euler derivatives. A fixed logarithmic cutoff yields weighted L1 and pointwise bounds uniform in R>0, retaining the support cost m^(-A). The shared-window consumer first fixes an indicator box, avoiding an invalid exchange of quantifiers. It does not supply R=0, a measurable selection in R, or uniformity over unbounded input seminorms. The standard Fourier/Schwartz foundations and the different inspected Mathlib versus requested revision remain disclosed.
