## Accepted Poisson, scalar, initial-sieve, retained-window and Dirichlet audits

The [five-packet frontier](QRH_POISSON_INITIAL_RETAINED_DIRICHLET_FRONTIER_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `0E6771676433E60D6622A15DC112BB3BDB15B4D466BDC6EF2DA87665996FAB50`, consolidates original trace-normalized Poisson, the selected quadratic scalar majorant, the initial cubic sieve, actual retained windows and the ordinary Dirichlet export bridge. Its [independent synthesis review](../qrh-campaign/agents/audit/REVIEW_QRH_POISSON_INITIAL_RETAINED_DIRICHLET_FRONTIER_2026-11-11.md), SHA256 `C4AD45A8F77994820A7550AAD9CC65CD67F92DCFDA2234F41227A1F88913A012`, passed without correction. The ten author/reviewer hashes are recorded there. These are bounded source/mathematical implications, not compiled proofs or complete provider validation.

Original Poisson now has an inspected derivation through actual product-lattice summability, affine determinant and full dual-frequency reindexing. The paper Fourier normalization absorbs the covolume factor, radial scaling gives K/|c|^2, masks retain one divisor denominator, and unit-generator changes are justified inside the full radial sum. The original square contributes Z^-D. Genuine summability precedes finite/infinite rearrangements and the separate zero-mode split. The remaining local Gauss, coordinate/ray and standard library foundations are explicit.

The scalar estimate accounts for all nine positive terms, whose allowances sum to99921<262144. The nested product budgets give256eta and the selected shape adds3eta, for259eta; the annular divisor loss gives260eta. The explicit K>=1, q<=X, fixed-profile and constant-order conditions remain. Arbitrary nonnegative scalar constants are not promoted to valid analytic estimates. The initial cubic proof independently obtains C0*(M+N^2) from the actual Gram pair, off-diagonal-only zero removal, normalized Gauss magnitude, scale-uniform lattice bound and finite ideal count. Its separate dyadic convergence uses only the initial bound and works for M>0,N>=1, without uniformity as M tends to zero.

The retained-window proof uses actual support and nonzero-block clipping to obtain full-puncture canonical states and both margins. It retains two normalization costs totaling4eta and the physical11eta cost, yielding m+15eta+piLoss+eps. It pays source fibers, quotient mass, units, subsets and rays, and supplies weighted integrability before the common integral bound. Degree/pool/clipping choices precede q, while C and Z0 may depend on q. The adopted terminology clarification is that CanonicalRankMoments is a quantified bound predicate on normalizedColumnEnergy, not its defining declaration. No formula changes.

The export bridge constructs the actual norm pullback and exact level-3q quadratic twist, retains all imprimitive Euler factors and six-unit normalization, and continues their product identity away from0,1. The ordinary theorem handles Re(s)>=1; only7/8<Re(s)<1 uses the Hecke product. No preservation-of-nonprincipality claim is needed. Actual finite Euler deletion transports primitive-family information to all characters. The common-probe and energy production remain substantive dependencies, not hypotheses added to the final Goldbach root.

# Bounded binary compression and current cost decisions

The coordinator read the complete bounded clause compiler A43E9F4B and independent review F4B77426. Accept its finite algorithm and conditional cost guard. The no-increase statement is limited to its charged schedule: choose instances with E_i<=D_i and require C<=sum(D_i-E_i), including all indexing/specialization and repeated proof-environment costs. # Conductor precision and finite-polynomial interpolation decisions

2026-11-11. The preceding status turn completed root reading of two new independent reviews, providing evidence for the acceptance decisions below. This continuation changes the authoritative research plan. The end-to-end compute-minimization objective remains active and incomplete; no blocking condition is present.

## Accepted coarse schedule

Root read the conductor-specific schedule, its independent review and the corrected parameter-selection evidence. Accept `MINIMAL_THETA_NONPRINCIPAL_SCHEDULE_2026-11-11.md` and its bound JSON as the current coarse-value candidate. The independent join review passed 30,281 exact checks, including all 29,996 explicit conductor choices across four guard scenarios. This is an independently checked composition, not a new numerical evaluation.

The main scenario chooses theta64 for 3,876 conductors, intermediate theta for 3,613, and theta128 for 10. Its shorter theta128 transform lengths are preserved. Local seed precision ranges from 302 to 404 fractional bits; local time precision ranges from 238 to 340 before interpolation. These are sufficient local fractional-bit requirements, not full operand widths or a measured speedup. Shared caches still satisfy their strictest consumers.

The selected schedule retains 1,775,431,871,117 small-branch requested outputs and 38,066,330,390,620 disjoint nonprincipal requested outputs. It preserves all large-family work, m159 seed bands, setup aliases and eleven required-unpriced records. No heterogeneous arithmetic grand total is meaningful. The mandatory bucket correction retains rounded additions and their error allowance; the original exact-bucket sentence is not accepted.

## Accepted interpolation option

Root read the finite-polynomial interpolation author and independent review, then the minimal-theta applicability supplement and its independent review. Accept this as a conditional paper construction for every selected integer theta from64 through128. It supplies a complete in-domain interpolation bound without the old unresolved sufficiently-large-height condition.

The interpolated function is the exact finite real polynomial T of the selected row's certified coefficient centers. The target analytic/coefficient error is added once. It is not amplified as if the grid samples were values of the analytic function. Only T has periodic index wrapping; no periodicity of the L-function is assumed. New theta values require their own coefficient certificates even when array dimensions agree.

The conservative option uses 340 nodes and seven rational kernels, with norm below4. It divides each selected row's time, omega and final time-output rounding tolerance by16, sufficient for four additional fractional bits at those stages. Other stages retain their own requirements. Thus the inherited 238--340 time range becomes a sufficient 242--344 range for this option; this does not describe every required arithmetic width. The resulting target error is below15epsilon/16. All N polynomial samples used by a wrapped stencil must meet the arithmetic bound; the old exported Y prefix alone is insufficient.

Charge the extra work separately from the accepted coarse JSON. The dense seven-offset direct-stencil example has 12,427,893,758,063 new target points and 4,225,483,877,741,420 weighted real terms. These are conditional schedule counts, not a forecast that all such points will be needed. Numerical Y outputs need the separately proved positive rescaling. Sign-only consumers may omit that rescaling through the exact positive-factor identity, while retaining canonical phases, inverse-character channels, central evidence and Turing obligations.

This accepts a feasible interpolation option, not a cheapest implementation. A raw-polynomial sign interface, shorter conductor-dependent stencils, and dense/polyphase alternatives remain separate research candidates. None is silently substituted into the coarse JSON or the accepted interpolation count.

## Reciprocal generation and verification

Root read the dyadic reciprocal proposal, mandatory product-width clarification and independent review. Accept the combined paper error proof and conditional price comparison. The valid setup product bound is A*v<=2^142 and may require143 magnitude bits. Bounded unaccepted inputs still require the wider153-bit check; the original strict bound is rejected.

No unconditional verification saving is credited. A reciprocal hint can accelerate production while the final checker uses the original exact small-denominator nearest-rounding identity. This preserves the narrower checker option. Approximate reciprocal checking, hint generation, cache reuse and exact quotient checking have different prices; eliminating a producer division does not establish a cheaper proof check.

# Shorter stencils and raw sign decisions

2026-11-11. Research progress after the conductor-precision decision. Root fully read the three author reports and independent reviews below. Accept their conditional mathematical constructions and typed operation comparisons. The end-to-end objective remains active; actual signs, completeness, finite coverage and ordinary-rule implementation remain outstanding.

## Shorter interpolation stencils

Accept the variable-order finite-polynomial construction. The exact bound pi*J/N <160919/393216 applies to every selected row because N>=2^18. For each of 130 theta/conductor-cohort pairs, choose the smallest positive even r with M*q^r<epsilon/16. The independent review reconstructs all 130 orders, all 280 exact kernel norms and all 29,996 row admissions and schedules. Its 93,632 exact checks passed. All 40 even orders182 through260 occur, and each of the seven offset norms is below4.

The complete target error remains below15epsilon/16 with the existing scaled-T sample budget. Periodic wrapping belongs only to the actual finite polynomial; its source-bound coefficient array and the closed physical target interval are unchanged. This adds no analytic halo, zero-spacing or sign-success premise.

For the main dense independent-stencil comparison, real products fall from4,225,483,877,741,420 to2,439,308,097,734,074. The reduction is exactly1,786,175,780,007,346 products, about42.3 percent of that category. It is not an end-to-end speedup or a forecast that all these queries are needed. Setup grows from2,380 to61,880 exact rational weights. Sparse uses may build only queried order/offset pairs, but must charge their actual proofs, rounding certificates and any reconstruction after eviction. The accepted option is chosen only when its complete setup and per-query prices justify it.

## Raw polynomial sign interface

Accept the separately reviewed raw-U interface, where T=omega*U and the actual unnormalized time DFT evaluates U. Its strict tests use the rational radius109*epsilon*P/704. Positivity of omega and the exponential Y factor transfers those certified strict signs to the actual canonical Hardy function. A sign of U without the translated radius is insufficient.

The intrinsic time-gate tolerance divided by8 suffices for this raw interpolation option, requiring three extra fractional bits relative to the selected coarse tolerance. It removes final omega scaling and positive Y scaling only for consumers using the new sign interface. It does not fulfill the old numerical-Y export contract. Larger raw integer parts, threshold handling, all-N stencil dependencies, fixed phases and separate count/central/repair obligations remain charged.

The independent review passed207 exact checks. Main coarse-prefix deductions are1,775,431,871,117 omega scalings and the same number of final Y products,3,791,938,303 shared positive-scale updates, and7,499 positive exponential seeds. Radial seeds remain. These are conditional typed deductions; no net time improvement is inferred, and no prefix saving is added again as a full-array saving.

The selected-plan consumer audit is being frozen separately. A typed sign-only metadata successor is in preparation; the immutable coarse Y schedule is not edited. Composition with the shorter stencils is also a separate pending supplement, so this decision does not silently assign both sets of savings to one unreviewed implementation.

## Dense alternatives

Accept the reviewed signed-frequency padded and polyphase identities, normalized-FFT error proof and priced break-even rule at their stated scope. The independent review passed9,843 exact checks. For all seven new offset classes, a matched preexecution polyphase schedule replaces12N padded-FFT butterflies with7J coefficient-phase products and their setup. If a base transform is already sunk, it cannot be subtracted from a fresh padded transform's price.

The reviewed analytic admission in that arithmetic packet explicitly uses theta64 or128. Any application to intermediate selected tilts must join their own reviewed analytic admission and exact precision allocation. Root does not infer a fully joined minimal-theta dense schedule merely from the polynomial identities. No dense route is selected without its setup, root certificates, storage, traffic and actual query distribution.

An adaptive switching theorem is awaiting independent review. It compares fixed evaluator schedules for the same realized requests, with controller and cache work charged; it is not a global optimum over different sign-search strategies.

## Execution and evidence

The workers briefly stopped with usage-limit errors. After the user reported restoration, root resumed the unfinished tasks from saved artifacts. The complete variable-order author packet survived and was independently reviewed without regenerating it. No build, function evaluation, numerical transform, prime scan or source/dependency edit occurred.

# Selected raw signs, shorter stencils and adaptive evaluation

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting independently reviewed smaller stencils and the raw sign interface. This turn accepts their reviewed composition and the source-level consumer change, and admits a conditional adaptive scheduling theorem. The full compute-minimization goal remains active and incomplete.

## The selected small branch can change its export contract

Root read the full selected-consumer audit, re-read the authoritative count decision and inspected the actual GRHUpTo source definition. The selected primitive nonprincipal branch needs finite RH, not amplitude-valued Y exports. Main critical-line lower counts and auxiliary rational weights use actual strict signs and rational endpoints. The canonical Gamma/logarithmic upper-count arithmetic is separate. Accept the consumer audit's conclusion at this paper/source scope.

The replacement must be a new raw-U sign/interpolation API. It does not satisfy the old promise to export Y at absolute tolerance tau after deleting Y scaling. Raw numerical centers and radii remain internal inputs to interpolation; sign-only export does not mean sign-bit-only arithmetic. The successor ledger must retain the radial seed, omega's frequency/root role, all fixed phases and coefficients, every consumed raw time output and all canonical count obligations.

The selected U_count<K+2 gate uses a critical-line-only K and the full closed zero carrier. Central contribution zero is a valid lower bound, not a claim of nonvanishing. General complex clusters cannot enter K without additional critical-line localization. Multiplicity, contour and unsuccessful-sign repairs retain separate actual-function contracts and costs. The principal1e8 branch is unchanged. No actual signs or successful surplus are supplied by this consumer audit.

## Raw signs and variable order compose

Root read the composition, the author's mandatory table erratum and its independent review. Accept the combination. The same fixed-phase coefficient array defines U; H*C_U<M transports the improved-bandwidth remainder into raw units. Every selected order182 through260 retains the reviewed norm below4. The old time tolerance divided by8 remains sufficient, and the exact sign radius stays109*epsilon*P/704.

At order r, use v_raw<=epsilon/[16*r*H*(C_U+3)] for weight and query rounding, or the proved sufficient conservative scaled-T tolerance. The circuit charges r products, r-1 additions and2r arithmetic rounds per query. Larger raw integer parts and exact product widths remain implementation costs. No additional scaling deduction follows from composing the two improvements.

The independent composition review passed1532 exact checks. The +320 additions count is2436206631058893; the original note's transcribed cell is rejected in favor of its separate erratum and already-correct receipt. All other schedule cells and the mathematical proof remain unchanged. The full sign-only operation-ledger successor is being checked separately; no frozen Y ledger is changed here.

## Adaptive sparse-to-dense scheduling

Root read the online policy and its independent review. Accept its conditional fixed-price theorem: after accounting for controller work, common work C and dense per-query work D, the cost is at most C+D+2*min(R,K), where R is the nonnegative excess all-local cost and K buys a persistent dense result. Switch before the next local expense would cross K. This prevents a large first-use kernel or cache miss from creating an unbounded overshoot.

The proof retains all work already spent and applies at every stopping prefix of the same realized query sequence. It does not compare different sign-search strategies or give a global factor-two bound against arbitrary shared caches, padded transforms, changing precision or evicted arrays. Prices, nonnegative rents, persistent storage and the evaluator contract must be justified. The independent review passed58129 exact assertions; no runtime improvement is inferred from those abstract checks.

## Next cost decisions

The sign-only successor ledger is pending its independent join review. A half-size complex time transform for the exact Hermitian frequency array is under research, with a separate rounding/count review. This frequency symmetry must not merge distinct character channels. A full tilt/transform-length frontier is also under research, because the shortest transform is not automatically the cheapest at its required precision. A prioritization review is comparing these opportunities with the much larger retained categories and end-to-end unknowns.

All actual finite arithmetic, sign margins, count completion, principal certification, binary coverage, the prime ladder, analytic foundations and standard-rule checker acceptance remain open. No final core-hour estimate, Lean/MM0 build, numerical transform or function/prime evaluation occurred.

# Half transforms, full tilt choices and the next large cost target

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn adopted the independently reviewed sign-only ledger. This turn accepts the half-transform construction and its row-specific applicability, accepts the full finite tilt-choice portfolio with its cost qualifications, and prioritizes a new large-branch circuit feasibility test. The full end-to-end objective remains active and incomplete.

## Half-transform option accepted

Root read the complete half-transform author, independent review, applicability supplement and its independent review. Accept the exact even/odd packing and conditional arithmetic replacement. It uses one normalized positive transform of length N/2, then an exact dyadic shift, to return the N real raw-U samples. Sparse packing adds J products,2J additions and3J+1 rounds. Both actual character channels remain.

The error proof is parametric in each row's coefficient bound B_row>=1. Its gate is exactly the accepted selected coarse tolerance divided by8, including the safety minimum. The uniform-B numerical receipt is not substituted for a row-specific proof. Packing, phase preparation and the normalized-transform error are included before the final shift multiplies both grid and error by N/2. Source coefficients may have finer denominators; their first product widths remain separately required.

The original independent review passed23144 exact checks. The applicability review verified nine pins and the exact composition with the selected raw-sign rows. On the saved theta128-length main inventory, the specified complex products change from89,858,217,607,168 to43,822,509,521,403, including packing. These are typed counts, not a runtime ratio. A joined minimal-theta half/polyphase cost interface is in preparation; the immutable full-transform JSON is not relabeled or mutated here.

## Full finite parameter portfolio accepted

Root read the all-theta author and independent review. The latter passed4,140,178 exact checks, including all130 parameter admissions and1,949,740 conductor/scenario/theta length tests. Accept the complete finite selector portfolio. It contains44,585 least-theta-per-length representatives across29,996 rows, each with one or two lengths.

There are18 verified exceptions where theta127 admits half the length required at both endpoints. For main conductor745, N127=2,097,152 while N64=N128=4,194,304. This does not invalidate the former smallest-theta-at-fixed-N128 result. It disproves treating N128 as a universal minimum length.

One representative per length is a valid cost reduction only for the stated common-envelope, monotone-precision and compatible-setup model. Without those hypotheses, preserve all65 theta choices. Raw integer widths, angle leaves, cache reuse and sign/repair behavior can differ. Shared setup can couple conductors, so rowwise minimization is not automatically globally optimal. No new production policy or all-minimum-length runtime winner is selected by this acceptance. New N changes the coefficient grid and requires fresh bound arrays.

## Prioritize larger categories

Root read the source-pinned prioritization report as planning evidence. The half-transform net saving is46,035,708,085,765 complex products before additional data/setup costs. The retained large character category alone has1,004,103,686,197,888 general complex products, and residue evaluation has1,139,279,177,111,712 real-times-complex Horner stages. Finish the bounded small half/polyphase join, then test the degree19 large residue polynomials on their arithmetic numerator grids. Prime estimates supplies exact bucket/height occupancy; moebius reviews it. Integration supplies the exact integer finite-difference block theorem; statement audit reviews it. Initialization, all traversed nonunits, final rounding and enlarged integer widths must be charged. The correct scaled coordinate is h=(2D_out*a-k*c)/(2c), even in the final band where source D4096 differs from output D8192.

The metadata reference initializes each bucket with20 polynomial values and190 differences. Its applicability and any saving remain pending. A root suggestion under consideration bounds polynomial-only initialization/extrapolation by |h|<3/4 when c>20*(2D_out); this would not assert analytic-function accuracy outside the output cell. No such alternative is adopted without its circuit/error review. Short or unprofitable cases retain Horner. This research is a falsifiable test of a large category, not a credited reduction.

# Newton initialization and coefficient-grid decisions

2026-11-11. Root has read the complete Newton author and independent review, the coefficient-grid author, its mandatory mixed-evaluator clarification and independent review, and the width-sensitive cost framework. Accept the two reviewed mathematical adapters at the conditional scopes below. No operation-ledger saving, kernel theorem, actual certificate or runtime improvement is admitted.

## Accepted Newton initializer

The degree-19 binomial-basis Horner recurrence initializes the exact forward differences using 190 general narrow-factor products, 357 complex additions and 357 shifted-operand occurrences. The old twenty-value/triangle initializer uses 380 general products and 570 additions/subtractions. Nineteen conductor-dependent coefficient prescales, factor setup, signed arithmetic and cache binding remain charged. A complex scale represents two coordinate products.

The proof tracks every partial Horner polynomial and bounds intermediate products before addition. Initialization updates descend; subsequent difference traversal updates ascend. The accepted domain remains the screened full-state circuit with c > 20F and the occupancy's ties-down anchors. Neither the artificial R > 10B product screen nor the new initializer alone extends that domain. The separate c > 10F extension awaits root review of its author and independent review.

For a bucket of length L, full-state initialization and traversal require 19L + 338 complex additions, versus 19L + 551 for the previous initializer. Nonunit advances remain included. These counts are not substituted into the separately pruned long-block schedule. The independent review checked 432 formal identities/counts/width specializations; the author checked 1,550. Neither checker evaluated actual analytic coefficients.

## Accepted optional b100/g100 coefficient adapter

An absolute coefficient-error contract does not prove a common denominator. The optional adapter explicitly rounds each of the twenty complex coefficient centers to grid 2^-100 and names a new polynomial. At consumed arguments |h| <= 1/4, this adds less than (4/3)2^-100; exact polynomial evaluation followed by one final coordinate rounding adds less than 2^-100. Their total fits within the previous (8/3)2^-100 per-residue allowance.

The all-adapter character-summed allowance is 1477u_H. A mixed schedule must use the clarification's subset-weighted bound, which is at most the old 1688u_H. Original Horner fallbacks retain the original coefficient array. The original first-four-band and final-band alpha error caps remain valid. The explicit source magnitude margins preserve C = 2^16 after conversion.

The optional b100 representation gives sufficient signed live/temporary widths of 499/500 for the pruned circuit and 500/501 for the c > 20F full-state/Newton circuit. The cancelled final-round divisor has at most 362 bits. This replaces the illustrative b122 widths only when the adapter or a direct grid-membership theorem is actually supplied. Charge twenty complex conversions per distinct retained coefficient array, actual source decoding and witnesses, cache reconstruction and binding. Conductor-dependent prescales remain separate. Independent review passed 2,906 exact checks; the author passed 1,915.

## Cost comparison still open

The width-sensitive framework correctly keeps the original rounded Horner stages separate from wider exact-integer operations. For the Newton full-state alternative, the uniform symbolic schedule is

    setup + 19B * prescale + 190B * general_product
      + (357B + 19A) * wide_add + 357B * shifted_operand
      + R * final_round + conversion + traffic_and_binding.

Prices must use the actual widths and the chosen objective: generation, proof construction or ordinary-rule checking. A new wide final quotient witness is not automatically cheaper than nineteen narrow Horner witnesses. The original-center fused rational Horner comparator must retain its own coefficient precision; the b100 adapter cannot silently reduce its costs without a separate error allocation.

The selected operation ledger continues to retain original Horner until a complete comparison and coverage policy pass. Integration and statement audit now own the joined comparison; major constants and moebius own the cost-aware occupancy frontier. Zero sum and prime estimates are checking conductor-specific fixed-divisor proof tables. Independent feasibility is reviewing the expanded domain. These are research tasks, not builds or numerical certificate generation.

Actual spectra and sign/count closure, principal certification, finite binary coverage and its prime ladder, analytic foundations, proof production and end-to-end calibration remain open. No final core-hour estimate is supplied. The compute-minimization goal remains active and incomplete.

# Extended residue domain and rounding-proof reuse

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting the reviewed Newton initializer and optional b100 coefficient adapter and updating the checkpoint. This turn root read both the extended-domain author and review, and both the fixed-denominator author and review. Accept the results below as conditional mathematical and arithmetic-proof designs. No numerical certificate, implementation, runtime saving or selected operation-ledger deduction follows.

## Broader full-state domain

The exact full-state difference method and its Newton initializer extend from c > 20F to c > 10F. Here F = 2Dout is the delivered output grid, including F = 16384 in the final band. The same fixed anchor and ties-down rule apply. If B_c counts all nonempty integer-numerator buckets, phi(c) > 10B_c implies the new guard. Replacing B_c by useful-unit buckets would require another argument.

Auxiliary polynomial arguments satisfy |h| < 6/5. The finite degree-19 geometric sum is less than 256, so live differences and raw Newton temporaries are below 2^43Q and traversal additions below 2^44Q. The Newton proof bounds unadded products using partial-degree invariants. Its interior order is at most 18, which absorbs the extra factor two. General factors still fit 19 magnitude bits in the actual conductor/grid family.

At the accepted optional b100 grid, sufficient signed live/Newton and traversal capacities are 506/507 bits, six above the old-guard 500/501. Coefficient prescales fit 479 signed bits, and the final cancelled divisor remains at most 362 bits. The actual output is still in its original cell with |h| <= 1/4. No actual-function approximation is required at auxiliary polynomial points, so the source and quantization error bounds remain unchanged. Mixed schedules retain the previous whole-band error cap.

Accept this as an additional admissible domain with its own widths. Keep the c > 20F option and its narrower widths available. No newly eligible conductor count or priced selection has been adopted. The author checked 18,166 exact cases and bounds; the independent review checked 40,059 and seven input pins. These are paper/metadata evidence, not kernel proofs.

## Fixed-denominator final-round proof

At b = g = 100 let Q = (2c)^19 and write an exact coordinate as I/(2^100 Q). A rounded output m/2^100 is certified by one signed product p = mQ, residual R = I - p, and the range -Q <= 2R < Q. The asymmetric range correctly handles positive and negative half ties. Two independent endpoint multiplications are unnecessary.

The existing ordinary-arithmetic digit-table schema applies to this conductor-fixed Q. Certified facts T[d] = dQ for radix-256 digits, together with the checked digit decomposition of |m|, prove Q|m| by prefix accumulation. Signed interpretation then proves mQ. This table is independent of the coefficient array, height, fine anchor and coordinate. Reuse is valid within an actual checked cache lifetime.

The in-cell magnitude bound gives |m| < 2^117. Q has at most 362 bits, table entries 370 bits and products 479 bits. A padded unsigned product therefore uses 15 quotient digits, 46 divisor digits, 47 table digits and 60 product digits. The last bound uses the sharper bit hypotheses; arbitrary 46-by-15-digit products need not fit 60 digits.

Under the explicitly declared PA macro-event model, the table product subtotal is 1723 events versus 3481 rectangular schoolbook events, with setup 24739. Its first strict subtotal crossover is 15 scalar products, or 8 complex outputs. These are counts of different lemma/table events, not equal-time instructions. Terms, decomposition, theorem unification, signed residual/order checks, transport, cache lifetime and proof storage remain charged. Known powers of two can be removed by either method and require a fair matched comparison.

Accept this as an optional proof-producing arithmetic method. Native quotient discovery still costs work. Lean's direct numeral multiplication is a separate comparator and may be preferable. Local MM0 proof resets and separate shards require rebuilding or checked theorem transport, not hash-only provenance. The independent review checked 30,580 exact cases and twelve pins; the author checked 67,725. Neither generated actual residue outputs or an ordinary proof implementation.

## Next comparisons

Two additional proposals now affect the comparison and remain unaccepted here. First, after the same b100 coefficient conversion, Horner can round each product and add the same-grid coefficient exactly. Its proposed arithmetic error is less than (4/3)u_H, leaving another (4/3)u_H for coefficient conversion within the old allowance. The author and independent reviewer must establish all widths and source bindings before this becomes a priced baseline.

Second, exact divisibility may shrink finite-difference states. Root identified full normalization by F^j j!, which simplifies Newton initialization but adds small-factor multiplications to traversal. Independent feasibility has a powers-of-two variant with shifted traversal and a cell-dependent common-factor reduction. Those variants need raw-width proofs and complete operation counts before adoption. The research team is comparing both against the stronger Horner candidate.

Principal certification, finite binary coverage and its prime ladder, actual spectral signs and counts, analytic foundations, and ordinary-rule proof construction remain open. They retain their previous unpriced status. No final core-hour estimate is supplied. The end-to-end compute-minimization goal remains active and incomplete.

# Priced residue frontier and a variable-grid proposal

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting the larger finite-difference domain and final-round proof reuse. This turn root read the complete joined arithmetic comparison and review, Newton cost frontier and review, uniform b100 Horner author and review, narrow-factor table author and review, and the independent review of root's variable-grid proposal. Accept these at their stated conditional mathematical/model/metadata scopes. No actual arithmetic certificate, kernel theorem or runtime saving follows.

## Accepted comparison and frontier

The joined comparison now accounts for the original Horner first coefficient's actual grid, subsequent narrower accumulators, coefficient alignment, all Newton degree-dependent products, prescales and shifted operands, nonunit traversal, output rounding, source conversion and checked cache lifetimes. The output numerator has a stronger in-cell 480-bit signed bound and need not be charged at the largest auxiliary-state width. The c > 20F and c > 10F methods retain their different width proofs. Root accepts this complete comparison interface, supported by 1,517 independent checks and the author's 852 checks.

The frontier independently reconstructs all forty guard cohorts and 720 projected price subsets. Its independent review passed 1,899,281 exact metadata checks; the author passed 2,065,413. It preserves all integer advances, including nonunits, and initializes every nonempty bucket in the stated reference schedule. Its baseline evaluates only units. Source-cache coupling remains outside any claim that independent per-conductor choices optimize a complete implementation.

For one conductor and active band, write r = phi(c), b for all nonempty buckets, a = c-1-b and w for its height count. In a declared uniform price model, the full saving is

    19wr C_H + D
      - w[190b C_G + (357b+19a) C_A + 357b C_S
          + 19b C_P + b C_K + r C_R] - T.

The terms include general products, wide additions, shifted operands, prescales, bucket setup and final rounds. T contains remaining differential setup, conversion, binding, traffic and proof costs; D is any separately justified baseline-only credit. Select the method only when the complete expression is positive. Exact degree-specific prices may replace these coarse prices. The pending stronger Horner circuit must supply its own C_H rather than inheriting an expensive old comparator.

The main scenario has candidates in the first two height bands under some optimistic price projections. The new third-band domain has no candidate even in the listed equal-general-product, otherwise-free projection. This is a restriction of this schedule and those prices, not a universal obstruction. Positive projected reserves still have to pay every excluded cost. No selected operation ledger or runtime estimate is changed by accepting the frontier.

## New root-authored precision proposal

Root derived and froze DEGREE_DEPENDENT_HORNER_GRID_PROPOSAL_2026-11-11.md. Its independent review passed without correction. It rounds original coefficients directly to degree-indexed grids b_0 = b_1 = 100 and b_j = 100-j for 2 <= j <= 19. Each product rounds to its output stage's grid, followed by an exact coefficient addition on that grid. Final output remains grid100. The argument bounds each complex coordinate round by (3/4)2^-b_j and exploits |h| <= 1/4 to obtain total coefficient-change plus arithmetic error below (21/8)2^-100, within the old (8/3)2^-100 allowance.

The proposal includes signed intermediate bounds, exact 0/2/1-bit stage alignment, direct original-center conversion and cache binding. Its scalar script passed 92 rational/width/source-pin checks; the independent reviewer passed 148 checks using descending error recurrences and separate recursive magnitude envelopes. The sum of coefficient fractional precisions is 1811 instead of 2000, but this is not a cost ratio. Native limb counts can stay unchanged. Integration will price the actual two multiplication witnesses per coordinate and the remaining operations. Accept the mathematical error/width adapter, with direct source conversion and all proof/cost obligations retained.

The uniform b100 Horner circuit also passed independent review, with 1,047 checks against the author's 9,370. Accept its same-grid exact additions and product-only rounding as another comparator. It uses 117/118-bit signed coefficient/accumulator capacities and 136/137-bit raw/witness temporaries. Its twenty source conversions remain charged per distinct retained array. It is valid on every original unit target without a finite-difference conductor guard.

The narrow-factor proof-table comparison passed independent review with 69 checks. Its growing-prefix table costs 337 versus 232 events per product under the declared byte-template prices. Setup is additional, so reuse cannot rescue that particular subtotal. This is not a general lower bound on constant-multiplication proofs or an elapsed-time claim. The review corrects one documentary reference: the rectangular template is in section 3 of its pinned source, not section 2. Accept the comparison with that clarification.

Normalized finite-difference states, including powers-of-two and factorial variants, remain under author/reviewer work and are not included in the accepted frontier counts. During that work the team found an occupancy prose discrepancy concerning the even-conductor height slope. The report's script uses the expected 1063200/c even slope, while one prose clause states 531600/c for both parities. A separate source/receipt provenance review and clarification are underway. No accepted count is changed here, and no new normalized occupancy metadata is accepted before that check.

## Remaining objective

No actual coefficients, signs, zero counts, prime certificates, builds or source declarations were produced. Actual spectral acceptance, principal certification, finite binary coverage and its ladder, analytic foundations, ordinary-rule proof construction and calibrated end-to-end costs remain open. The research objective remains active and incomplete. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Normalized and rounded residue circuits

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting stronger Horner comparators and a complete conditional cost frontier. This turn root read the complete normalization author and independent review, rounded-initial-state author and review, optimized-grid author and review, and the independent height-provenance review. Accept the mathematical circuits and bounded metadata below at their stated scopes. No selected ledger deduction, concrete ordinary proof, numerical certificate or runtime improvement is admitted.

## Exact normalization

For c = 2^s w with w odd and F = 2^f, cancel the common cell factor 2^t from v = 2c, F and kc, where t = min(f,s+1,s+nu2(k)). The completed polynomial numerator and denominator lose the factor 2^(19t). Partial Newton degree i instead loses 2^(it); prescales must be constructed for the reduced polynomial rather than uniformly dividing every old prescale by the final factor.

The reduced order-j difference is divisible by 2^lambda_j, where lambda_j = j(f-t)+nu2(j!). Storing the quotient gives a normalized Newton recurrence with oddpart(j) small factors and ascending traversal by shifted additions. The shifted higher operand can be wider than its stored encoding. The separately proved full-factorial normalization removes those small initializer factors but adds small scales in traversal. Its 167 initializer-addition saving versus 21 additional additions per advance is only a same-price subtotal comparison.

Accept the per-order and raw-width formulas, including separate delta=0 and delta>=1 cases, without replacing them by a single maximum-width price. Some cells have an additional unit-output factor when s=f and k is odd; it cannot be removed from the all-numerator traversal. Horner receives the same target fraction reduction. Factoring an odd denominator core can reduce a multiplication operand but does not justify discarding dividend bits or narrowing residual operands. Tables must bind their actual reduced denominator or odd core, with checked lifetimes.

The final normalization receipt, ending 0AE9F335, passed 9,568 checks; independent review passed 280,021, including formal state comparisons and direct reconstruction of the clipped valuation classes. The preliminary receipt beginning 33BBAA6C is withdrawn. The independent provenance review confirmed that accepted occupancy prose, code and counts already used the correct even-conductor height slope. The error belonged only to the new unpublished normalization draft. Root's earlier attribution correction remains mandatory; no old count needs repair.

## Round initial differences once, then add exactly

This new alternative consumes the same explicit b100 polynomial. Compute all twenty exact initial differences, round each once to grid 2^-p, and traverse by exact dyadic additions. For bucket length L <= 260, define

    S(L) = sum_(j=0)^min(19,L-1) binom(L-1,j),
    p = 102 + bitlength(S(L)).

Pascal's identity gives the error in every live order as a binomial combination of the initial errors. The maximum row sum is S(L), so every live-order error remains below u/4, where u = 2^-100. Final rounded outputs never feed back into the state. Coefficient conversion below 4u/3, propagated initialization error below u/4 and final dyadic rounding below u total 31u/12 < 8u/3. Mixed schedules retain the old character-summed cap of 1688u.

Quantitative geometric slack proves sufficient live/addition capacities of p+44/p+45 in the c > 10F domain and p+38/p+39 under c > 20F. The maximum p is 197 at L=260, yielding 241/242 and 235/236 signed bits respectively. This reduction applies after exact initialization and its conversion. All nonunit advances and all twenty initial conversions remain charged.

The initialization is expensive. To round I_j/(2^100 v^19) at grid p, the witness uses the shifted numerator I_j*2^(p-100) and product M_j*v^19. A conservative raw comparison can require 605 signed bits. Twenty complex wide conversions replace the old per-unit arbitrary-denominator output conversion. Each new unit output uses a dyadic rounding certificate, which still needs signed splitting, tie tests and binding. The complete break-even inequality retains the twenty wide conversions, smaller traversal additions, dyadic outputs, simultaneous state storage and precision/cache setup.

The author passed 10,937 exact checks. Independent review passed 106,317, including all full twenty-by-twenty error-propagation matrices through length 260 and raw signed rounding checks. Accept this conditional circuit, not a claim that it beats Horner or exact normalized differences.

## Bounded Horner precision optimization

The reviewed search uses direct original-center conversions, b0=100, monotone integer grids down to at least 70, and product-only rounding followed by exact additions. All profiles retain the same complete source-error contract. Its objective is explicitly the two unsigned byte-schoolbook product proofs per coordinate and stage. Both the first coefficient input and the rounded product use consistently sharpened bounds.

The minimum of that bounded objective is 15,442 events, compared with 16,252 for the earlier linear profile and 17,632 for uniform100 under the same width model. The minimum grid, ordered by degree, is

    [100,100,100,100,97,95,95,95,89,87,
     87,87,81,79,79,79,71,71,71,71].

Its error envelope is 5253u/2048, leaving 625u/6144 below the old allowance. It requires seven nonzero alignment shifts, totaling 29 bits and reaching eight bits. Keep the full 74-profile cost/error frontier rather than declaring this one vector optimal for a complete backend. Conversion, exact additions, signed residuals and comparisons, quotient discovery, theorem terms, cache traffic and source bindings are excluded from the optimized subtotal.

The author's forward dynamic program passed 1,767 checks. An independently implemented backward suffix program reproduced all 74 frontier weights and passed 4,802 checks. Accept completeness only within the stated monotone grid domain and arithmetic objective. A PA macro event is not a Lean reduction, MM0 instruction or unit of elapsed time.

## Next proof and price work

The complete normalized-FD/Horner comparison has been frozen and sent for independent review; root has not yet read that pair, so no acceptance is implied here. The rounded-initial-state price comparison is now assigned separately.

Root also derived two potential refinements, both pending. A finite positive Stirling envelope may bound every live difference much more sharply than the geometric support estimate. Truncating Newton to min(20,L) orders and pruning near the cell endpoint may keep every support point inside the cell, avoiding the conductor guard and reducing initialization/advance work for short cells. Authors and independent reviewers must establish these claims before any widths, domains or counts change. The accepted conservative circuits remain valid without them.

Actual spectral signs/counts, principal certification, finite binary coverage and its ladder, analytic foundations, ordinary-rule implementations and calibrated end-to-end costs remain open. The compute-minimization objective remains active and incomplete. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Sharper widths, complete prices and all-cell traversal

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting normalized and rounded residue circuits and the bounded Horner grid optimization. This turn root read the complete author/review pairs for the sharp Stirling bound, matched normalized costs, rounded-initial costs and truncated all-cell traversal. Accept the following conditional mathematical, metadata and cost interfaces. No actual coefficients, ordinary-rule implementation, numerical certificate, selected ledger deduction or runtime saving is claimed.

## Sharp difference bounds

The finite identity for the jth difference of x^k gives a positive Stirling-number envelope for every degree at most 19, |h| <= 1/4 and |step| <= 1/20. Twenty exact integer comparisons establish that every difference is bounded by 4C/3 when coefficient norms are at most C. The zero-polynomial case is handled separately. This finite-algebra theorem needs no analytic approximation at supporting points outside the cell.

The same envelope bounds partial Newton states after their degree-specific normalization. Separate sums of absolute product bounds control every raw Newton product and insertion, and adjacent-order envelopes control actual traversal additions before cancellation. With C=2^16 and the reviewed one-time initialization-rounding error, both rounded stored states and actual raw traversal additions fit p+18 signed bits, at most 215 at p=197. Exact b100 initialization fits 480 signed bits; the largest initial-round comparison fits 579 rather than 605.

Accept these as improved sufficient capacities in the existing c > 10F domain. The operation counts, 31u/12 evaluation allowance, forty coordinate initialization-round witnesses and final dyadic certificates remain. The author checked 576 finite identities/bounds; the independent review checked 3,334, including all partial degrees, raw operations and length-dependent widths. Old conservative width reports remain valid and unchanged.

## Complete comparisons retain conversion and cache costs

The matched normalized-FD/Horner comparison passed 10,518 independent checks. It gives Horner the same target fraction cancellation as FD, including the exceptional unit-output factor. It prices the arithmetic numerator and quotient-witness product separately, uses input and output stage precisions correctly, and retains all shifted normalized advances. Full-denominator tables require the actual cell/output variant key; odd-core tables require their own exact shift adapter. Distinct degree-grid coefficient arrays do not share b100 conversion work automatically.

The rounded-initial cost comparison passed 126,116 independent checks. Its explicit adapter from normalized E_j to the initial dyadic quotient uses E_j*2^(p-100+lambda_j)/D, with only proved power cancellation. All twenty complex initial conversions remain charged. The signed nearest-round scheme proves a product and three additive identities, with nonnegative range witnesses. Signed bridges, shifts, decompositions, theorem terms, quotient discovery and bindings remain additional named costs.

Its padded 362-bit-denominator example requires 292,000 initial conversion-core events at L=260. Each exact final conversion costs 7,580 core events per unit versus 218 for the dyadic alternative. Forty units are needed to pay the initial conversion core in that example. This is not a total break-even threshold: traversal, setup, signs, terms and actual denominator reductions can change it. At L=20, that same conversion-only threshold is 28, above the cell length. Neither example establishes a method choice.

Accept the full price inequalities and normalized-initializer adapter. The price paper deliberately uses its older conservative widths; the new Stirling capacities require an explicit matched reprice. Its best-Horner comparator includes the reviewed precision frontier, but the 15,442 product subtotal is not a complete Horner cost. Common setup cancels only for identical retained source/proof objects. The differences between generation, proof construction, checking, storage and traffic remain explicit.

## All-cell truncated and pruned circuit

The new theorem retains m=min(20,L) initial differences and shrinks the live order range near the cell endpoint. Newton order j depends only on old orders j and j-1, so discarding higher orders preserves every retained partial state exactly. The degree-19 polynomial and all twenty source coefficients remain. This is not polynomial truncation.

Every retained difference support lies inside the physical cell. Thus the proof no longer requires c > 10F or c > 20F. It separately bounds partial-degree raw products from the in-cell endpoints, including the singleton case where no step-size bound is needed. Ascending traversal reads every required upper entry before discarding it. All nonunit positions remain part of that traversal.

General initializer products and recurrence additions number G(m)=m(39-m)/2. Small-scale counts include j=1 through m-1, each occurring 20-j times. Pruned traversal uses L(L-1)/2 additions for L <= 20 and 19L-190 for L >= 20. Only m complex initial differences need rounding, while all twenty source coefficient conversions remain separately charged per distinct array.

The truncated error matrix gives the same propagated bound below u/4 and complete 31u/12 evaluation allocation. At L=260, the conservative all-cell rounded live/add capacities are 234/235 signed bits and the largest initialization comparison is 598 bits. These differ from the sharp 215-bit result, which currently has its separate small-step guard. Do not assign the sharp widths to every all-cell instance without proving that additional condition or another bound.

The author checked 17,528 formal/scalar statements. Independent review checked 1,948,671, including all short-cell cases and pruned error matrices. Accept the all-cell circuit and exact count formulas. Domain admission does not imply a later-band saving; singleton cells can still favor Horner. A whole-family candidate operation ledger is being derived from existing length histograms, with independent review assigned.

## Next evidence

The stored Hurwitz coefficients may support much smaller degree-specific magnitude bounds than C=2^16. The author/reviewer work must bound the actual finite translated coefficients plus their numerical errors, rather than substituting true fine-anchor derivatives for the stored centers. Repricing fixed Horner profiles under those bounds is assigned separately. No such improvement is credited here.

A conservative residue-evaluator implementation blueprint is also being prepared. It will distinguish generic soundness lemmas, concrete arithmetic witnesses, source bindings, cache keys and coverage requirements in Lean and ordinary PA/MM0. It is a handoff document, not new declarations or kernel closure.

Actual spectral signs/counts, principal certification, finite binary coverage and its prime ladder, analytic foundations, arithmetic implementations and end-to-end calibration remain open. The compute-minimization goal remains active and incomplete. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Stored coefficient bounds and candidate operation ledger

2026-11-11. Research resumed after agent restoration. Root read the complete stored-coefficient review, all-cell candidate ledger and independent review, and the conservative evaluator blueprint V2. The stored-coefficient author had already been read in full. Root checked 50 source-path hash entries, including all 26 blueprint pins, with no mismatch. Accept the conditional mathematical bounds, exact metadata and implementation handoff below. No actual arithmetic certificate, Lean/MM0 theorem, method selection or runtime saving is established.

## Stored coefficients support narrower operands

The magnitude argument bounds the actual finite translated coefficient vector and then adds the numerical error of its stored centers. It does not identify those centers with the true fine-anchor derivatives. The original analytic shift bias and target Taylor remainder remain separate obligations.

For positive degree k, the first four source bands have ideal magnitude at most 6(5/4)^k/k!. The final band has ideal magnitude at most (15/2)(7/8)^k/k!, including its actual postscale from source normalization 4096 to output normalization 8192. The numerical additions differ between these classes. In the first four bands the per-slot error uses the minimum of a weighted-vector consequence and a flat translation bound. In the final band the inherited eta radius already includes translation, postscaling and its output round. Degree zero has a separate finite Euler-Maclaurin bound.

The independent review checked 485 exact assertions, including source domains, quarter-shift orientation, endpoint cases, the m159 remainder and the distinction between finite-vector and true-jet errors. Accept these magnitude envelopes for their source-bound objects.

Direct coefficient conversions and product rounding give a degree-specific Horner magnitude recurrence. The large degree-zero term enters only the final addition. At degree 19, the reviewed sufficient magnitude powers are -48 for the first four bands and -57 for the final band. At grid precision 71 these require at most 24 and 15 signed input bits, respectively. Every profile still needs its own evaluation-error proof. A magnitude bound alone does not admit an arbitrary precision profile.

These bounds authorize a matched reprice. Both the arithmetic product and the quotient-witness product must use their actual widths. The shorter factor may change orientation in high degrees. Direct source conversion, shifts, signed identities, raw residuals, additions and proof construction remain charged. A separate degree-specific finite-difference refinement is assigned so that Horner does not receive a source-bound advantage denied to its comparator. No new product subtotal is adopted in this decision.

## All-cell candidate counts are now explicit

The accepted truncated Newton circuit retains min(20,L) differences for a cell of length L and discards unused trailing orders during traversal. The polynomial remains degree 19. The new ledger folds this recipe into the existing height-weighted length histograms, with no conductor or unit rescan. Empty endpoint slots do no work; nonempty cells remain charged even if they contain no required unit output.

For the main height alternative the complete candidate has:

| Operation | Count |
|---|---:|
| Coefficient prescale products | 69,836,382,908,113 |
| General initialization products | 418,829,993,692,141 |
| Initialization additions, including binary small scales | 698,436,179,615,681 |
| Shifted operand occurrences | 661,655,619,722,181 |
| Pruned traversal additions | 1,698,782,723,254,662 |
| Initial complex conversions | 36,731,422,115,040 |
| Final dyadic complex conversions | 59,962,061,953,248 |

Each complex operation carries its stated coordinate implementation. These categories cannot be added as equal-cost operations. Initial conversion counts differ from the twenty original coefficient conversions required per distinct shared source array. That source union remains a separate cost.

The independent review reconstructed 40 cohorts, 2,373 length/precision rows, twenty band rows and four alternative totals in 41,375 exact checks. Accept those counts and their typed interpretation. The four alternatives remain separate schedules; none proves that the eventual sign and count closure succeeds.

The saved histogram lacks conductor values jointly with cell length, and lacks unit-output counts by length or precision. Its width tables therefore use declared conductor caps. Final output counts are exact, but final conversion prices require either an unresolved joint distribution or an explicitly conservative cohort maximum. Proportional allocation of units to length bins is not justified. All-cell validity does not establish profitability, especially for singleton cells.

## Conservative implementation handoff

Accept evaluator blueprint V2 as a research handoff. It separates true coarse jets, ideal finite translated coefficients and stored rounded centers. It lists ordinary arithmetic lemmas, concrete signed-rounding witnesses, source bindings, cache identities and complete output coverage for the admitted Horner and difference methods. This is not an approved declaration manifest or a formal dependency graph.

V2 deliberately uses the earlier conservative width and full-order difference variants. Its exclusion of subsequently reviewed Stirling, all-cell, normalized-initialization and degree-specific refinements does not revoke those separate results. Combining them into an implementation requires the stated adapters and domains. The predecessor's stronger possible reading of the coefficient source is superseded by V2's explicit finite-vector split.

## Current research and unresolved proof work

The fixed-profile Horner reprice is awaiting independent review. Degree-specific finite-difference initialization is assigned with a separate reviewer. Adaptive output Taylor order is also under author/reviewer investigation. It must retain the existing source and shift allowances and must not reuse degree-19 count formulas after changing the degree.

The updated end-to-end cost inventory is undergoing review. Its unconditional phrase about separate principal low-head moments has been corrected in an immutable erratum. The selected original-RH route has no separate N1500/Q1500 consumer; charge those statistics only when an actual producer or explicitly chosen alternate route uses them. No extra head requirement is adopted here.

The next decision needs complete method costs, including source conversion, initialization, signed witnesses, caches and transport. Local bit reductions alone do not settle that decision. Principal certification, finite binary coverage and its prime ladder, analytic libraries, successful spectral certificates and final assembly remain open. The compute-minimization goal remains active and incomplete. No core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Degree widths, shorter orders and complete cost priorities

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting stored-coefficient bounds and the exact all-cell candidate ledger. This turn root read the complete author/review pairs for fixed-profile Horner repricing, stagewise Newton widths and shorter Taylor orders, and the end-to-end cost inventory with its mandatory erratum and independent review. Root verified 52 and 39 unique source hashes in two batches, including overlap between them. Accept the results below at their stated conditional mathematical and metadata scope. No ordinary-rule implementation, numerical spectral certificate, runtime saving or completed theorem is claimed.

## Reprice both polynomial evaluators from the same coefficients

The independently reviewed Horner model now uses actual stored finite-coefficient envelopes, direct conversion errors and the incoming/output grids of each stage. It prices both the arithmetic product rM and the quotient-witness product Lv. The smaller byte operand is the outer row count in the declared rectangular multiplication template.

| Existing fixed profile | Earlier coarse-width subtotal | First four bands | Final band |
|---|---:|---:|---:|
| Uniform100 | 17,632 | 12,562 | 11,872 |
| Linear | 16,252 | 11,272 | 10,552 |
| Previously optimized vector | 15,442 | 10,702 | 9,970 |

These are product-template events per degree-19 complex evaluation. They are not executed MM0 instructions, Lean reductions or complete verification costs. The old vector remains cheapest among these three choices under this subtotal, but its old optimization result does not prove optimality for the new magnitude-dependent objective.

The independent review checked all 114 stages and six subtotals in 3,599 exact assertions. It also retained raw residual capacities before cancellation, the final degree-zero addition and direct source conversion. In the final fixed-vector case three two-byte variable products change orientation, saving twelve template events. The receipt field named guaranteed_cancelled_alignment_bits means the remaining alignment distance, as explicitly clarified in the frozen author and review.

Accept these matched product subtotals and width formulas. Full signed rounding, conversion, additions, shifts, proof references, caches and generation remain separate costs. A successor signed schedule has independently passed review but has not yet been read and adopted by root in this decision.

The matching Newton refinement uses the actual partial polynomial

    P_i(x) = sum_(ell=0)^i z'_(19-i+ell) x^ell.

Earlier stages therefore use only the appropriate coefficient tail. The large degree-zero source coefficient enters only the final order-zero insertion. Positive differences annihilate every polynomial term below their order, so that constant does not enter their magnitude bound.

The guarded finite-Stirling envelopes retain the c>10F or c>20F domain. The all-cell envelopes instead require each retained order j<L and use only physical in-cell support. Old-state products, the secondary scale, coefficient prescales and raw sums have separate bounds. None receives a smaller width by presuming numerical cancellation. Exact-state bounds cannot be assigned to rounded traversal states without adding their propagated error.

The independent review checked 1,260 state rows and 1,254 raw updates in 36,943 assertions. With the global v<=531600 example, completed exact states and raw sums need at most 476 signed bits. Initial coefficient capacities are 53 and 44 bits for the two source classes. These bounds improve the comparison inputs, but peak-bit ratios are not cost ratios. All twenty source conversions and nineteen prescales remain. Accept the source-bound widths and their separate domains, not a method winner.

## Shorter Taylor orders preserve the stated alpha allocation

For K coefficients, the actual continued Hurwitz function has sufficient character-summed target remainder

    T_K(t,D_out) = 1266 * ((abs(t)+K)/(4D_out))^K / K!.

The proof applies real Taylor's theorem to the actual function. It separately bounds the discrepancy between true fine coefficients and the retained finite translated coefficients. Prefix projection only removes terms from positive error sums, so the original source orders, full translations and numerical source allowances remain valid and fully charged.

The sufficient total remains T_K + analytic shift + numerical source/evaluation error < 2^-82. At the five declared height caps 500, 1000, 2000, 5000 and 7000, the admitted lengths are 19, 19, 19, 20 and 19. On the actual final requested grid, including the 640-node alternative, length 17 works through index 68945 and length 18 works through the last index 84839. The independent reviewer reconstructed all 95 height thresholds using exact integer roots, checked all four scenario endpoints and passed 2,629 assertions.

Accept the shorter-order implication at the exported 2^-82 alpha interface. It does not preserve the previously achieved final-band fraction 0.16 of that allocation. The reviewer traced the selected source, transform, interpolation and strict-sign interfaces and found no inspected sign formula spending that fraction elsewhere. This is interface compatibility only. There is no complete numerical selected-sign chain, and changed centers and radii require fresh strict acceptance and any necessary repairs. Old successful signs do not transfer automatically.

No source/translation work is removed here. A K-term Horner evaluator has K-1 stages, but its new top copy, precision and widths must be instantiated. Degree-19 finite-difference counts do not describe a degree-K-1 polynomial. A separate fixed-b100 prefix composition is now under review.

## Complete cost inventory and its correction

Accept the reviewed inventory as a conditional planning model, together with its mandatory principal-head erratum. The selected original-RH route has no separate N1500/Q1500 consumer requirement. Charge those statistics only if the actual chosen producer or an explicitly selected alternate route uses them. Principal RH through 10^8 itself remains required and unpriced.

The inventory preserves separate generation, proof construction, checking, bytes, memory, traffic and critical-path costs. Its eight exact sensitivity brackets passed independent review. For example, a complete residue evaluation costs more than the large-character product component when its average price exceeds about 16.75 large-product prices. That is a conditional comparison of defined components, not a claim that residue evaluation dominates all proof work.

The hypothetical maximum-step, all-fixed-root ladder count is corrected to

    44 * 247250000 - 103161663 = 10775838337

square calls. The earlier copied value was smaller by thirty. These synthetic labels are not a certified prime chain or an upper bound on real discovery/checking work. The frozen earlier report remains unchanged; the correction is mandatory for later comparisons.

Principal certification, finite binary coverage, the actual prime ladder, analytic libraries, failures/repairs and complete proof transport retain explicit unpriced terms. None is set to zero or displaced by residue micro-optimizations.

## New research decisions

Minimizing K at fixed precision is not necessarily the least-work choice. Root independently checked the exact scalar alpha inequalities for the three fixed shapes at K=18,19,20 with a common grid shift. For example, uniform K20 admits output precision 93 at all five selected caps, while the fixed vector at K20 requires precision 94 in the fourth band and 93 in the others. These are preliminary scalar screens, not adopted circuits or prices. The final cap in this screen is the actual maximum requested height 424195/64.

The joint-order/precision author and reviewer must preserve all source errors, prove any exact re-encoding to grid100, and recompute magnitudes where shifted grids fall below the old bound's precision-70 premise. One cheap additional tail stage may permit fewer precision bits on many expensive stages. No arbitrary-profile search or new source-production assumption is authorized by that observation.

Root also supplied a candidate exact cell-length guard for review. The ties-down formula bounds each member by 2kc-c+1 <= 2Fa <= 2kc+c. For L consecutive members this gives 2F(L-1)<=2c-1, so L>=11 implies c>10F. The assigned author/reviewer will check the actual source tie convention and use this only after proving its composition with the rounded circuit. This can connect length-only metadata to the guarded width bounds without a new conductor scan.

An independent branch now studies sorted word-mask streams for source-prime bitmap certification. It must prove exhaustive divisor progressions, merge consumption and source completeness, while charging record access and proof transport. It must not assume native mutable-memory acceptance. This addresses a separate finite-coverage cost risk.

The compute-minimization goal remains active and incomplete. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Joint precision and shared conversion decisions

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by accepting degree-specific widths, shorter-order mathematics and the corrected cost inventory. This turn root read the complete author/review pairs for signed Horner costs, fixed-grid prefixes and joint length/precision, then authored a concrete shared-conversion schedule and obtained independent review. Root verified 36 unique frozen artifact hashes in the adoption batch. Accept the conditional results below. No numerical sign certificate, ordinary-rule implementation, measured saving or final theorem is claimed.

## Signed checking now has an explicit schedule

For each real product round, check P=rM, V=Lv, R=X-V, S=2R+v and S+C+1=2v with natural S,C, followed by the exact coefficient addition. The identities imply -v<=2R<v, including negative nearest ties. Supplied slack encodings are independently bounded before the guard equality. Signed-magnitude branches, canonical zero and final carry matching remain required.

At degree19, this schedule has76 natural products,152 natural-addition instances,114 signed-add bridges,76 signed-product bridges and38 nearest-range implications. Its addition-template subtotals are3204/2964/2860 for uniform/linear/fixed profiles in the first four bands, and3068/2824/2716 in the final band. These supplement the previously reviewed multiplication subtotals. They do not price signs, range proofs, shifts, bindings, conversion, proof construction or traffic as zero.

The independent review passed4712 exact checks over228 stage/mode records. Accept the witness semantics, typed counts and complete symbolic price interface. Its optional one-bit divisor cancellation saves only a small number of addition events and must pay its own extra proof/binding costs. It is not an automatic improvement.

## Prefix counts and the joint choice

The fixed-output-grid prefix composition passed34515 independent checks. Constant lengths19/19/19/20/18 use1080290989644456 main-scenario Horner stages instead of1139279177111712. The difference is58988187467256 stages. The source generation, translations, transforms and successful sign counts are unchanged by this arithmetic comparison.

The deleted stages are cheaper than the old average. For the fixed profile, the first-class product subtotal changes10702 to10494 at K19; the final-class subtotal changes9970 to9656 at K18. Accept the exact prefix formulas, new top-copy error accounting and saved-total weighting. Do not infer a proportional evaluator or runtime reduction.

The joint study keeps the three existing grid shapes and tests their prefixes K10..20 with common output precisions90..100. Its complete error condition is

    target Taylor remainder + analytic shift + numerical source error
      + 633 * new conversion/evaluation error < 2^-82.

The old1688*2^-100 evaluation term is replaced, not added again. Source eta, full P27/P28 production and all330/350 translation updates remain. New magnitude bounds are computed directly even when a shifted coefficient grid falls below70. The old precision-70 accumulator table is not used there.

Independent review checked all2178 candidates in120672 assertions. Accept the admitted successor error contracts, exact grid100 re-encoding and bounded template comparisons. Re-encoding M/2^b as M*2^(100-b)/2^100 introduces no rounding error, but does not restore the old center or accuracy. New enclosures require new strict sign and completeness checks. The historical final fraction0.16 of the alpha budget is not retained.

For the fixed shape, useful candidates now include:

| Domain | K | Output precision | Product events | Addition events |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| First three band tops |19|94|9684|2616|
| Fourth band top |20|94|9858|2688|
| Actual final height range, shorter option |18|94|8932|2420|
| Actual final height range, extra-term option |19|93|8858|2448|

These are candidates for complete pricing, not an adopted production policy. In the final range, the extra term has only74 product-event prices minus28 addition-event prices available to pay its extra conversions, bridges, bindings and export work. At equal event prices the reserve is46. In the first three bands, retaining K20 at precision93 saves ten products but adds forty addition events versus K19 at precision94. This is why minimizing the multiplication count alone gives the wrong template choice there.

## Shared coefficient conversion has a concrete occurrence bound

Root's new source-bound schedule produces and directly converts one full fine-anchor layer at a time, retaining it while all active conductors at that height consume one chosen profile. Every required anchor is present, including clipped endpoints. The full-grid count includes unused anchors and is not claimed to be the minimum used-source union.

With fine denominators1024/2048/4096/8192/16384 and layer counts6401/6400/12800/38400/(20199+e), the main schedule has717688040 converted-array objects. Full K20 requires28707521600 coordinate conversions. Constant K19/19/19/20/18 requires27239446690. Those counts are about0.000479 and0.000455 conversions per required unit-residue input, respectively. These are ratios of occurrences, not equal-cost operations or per-object reuse guarantees.

The same count applies to a different single admitted grid shape with those same lengths; each original coefficient is converted directly. A portfolio retaining multiple shapes must charge its actual union of converted objects. All twenty original delivered slots and their source-production cost remain. The conversion count is a separate category from source EM slots.

The stored coefficient bounds prove that directly converted numerators at grids0..100 fit signed114 bits. A declared signed128-bit payload therefore gives at most10486400 bytes for the full K20 converted layer, or9437760 bytes for the constant-prefix layer. The precision interval here is only a capacity statement, not analytic admission of every grid.

Independent review passed4216 checks covering all40 band/mode/scenario rows, exact totals, source bindings and capacity bounds. Accept the specified one-admission schedule and raw converted-payload bound. An actual checker must retain the corresponding checked context. Repeated admissions, worker copies, eviction, parsing and proof import are charged by their actual multiplicity. Original source encodings, proof objects, recurrence state, transform kernels/state, output arrays and transposition are excluded from the payload number. No whole-pipeline memory or stock-MM0 capacity claim follows.

## Next work targets complete costs

The cell-length guard and composed FD schedule have been authored and are under independent review. Root has read the author but adopts no new fixture cost here. A separate successor now generalizes rounded-initial differences to shorter polynomial degree and coarser output precision, using the actual3/4-ulp coordinate-nearest constant. It must replace, rather than silently inherit, the old internal u/4 error promise if its precision choice changes.

The large character-transform branch is receiving a matched arithmetic-width model so its roughly10^15 complex products can be compared with residue evaluation in common units. Saved-statistics discovery is separate from any new conductor inventory. The current candidate precision allocation is not yet a selected tolerance or complete sign budget.

The source-prime bitmap branch has a new sorted word-stream acceptance proposal under review. MM0 static-source work is also examining how the proposed digit-template events expand into actual commands and argument bindings. These tasks address costs outside the local Horner comparison.

Actual spectral acceptance, principal RH certification, finite binary coverage, the prime ladder, analytic libraries, ordinary-rule implementations and full resource calibration remain open. The compute-minimization goal remains active. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.
