# Transform and finite certificate cost decisions

2026-11-11. Root accepts four independently reviewed research packets at their stated conditional mathematical and arithmetic-model scope. Root has read the author/review reports and verified 25 frozen report, script and receipt hashes in this adoption batch. No production certificate, kernel theorem, selected precision, measured runtime saving or end-to-end estimate follows.

## Length guards and variable finite differences

The exact cell rule gives 2F(L-1) <= 2c-1, hence c > F(L-1). This supplies the strict guards needed for the sharper difference bounds at lengths at least 11 and 21. The degree19 fixture comparison and its complete typed schedules passed 726047 independent checks. Its numerical costs remain the old-grid reference, not the final method choice.

The successor permits K18..20, coefficient grids b93..100 and internal offsets d0..2 for L1..260. Put m=min(K,L), S=sum of binom(L-1,j) for j<m, and p=b+bitlength(S)+d. The live rounding error is beta=(3/4)S*2^-p. It replaces the old u100/4 promise. Every stored-state width must include beta and every two-state raw sum must include twice beta.

Finite polynomial divisibility proves that j!F'^j divides each initial difference. This permits power normalization without a c/F guard. Width bounds still need their own physical-support or guarded-kernel proof. All source generation and twenty-slot delivery remain. The full alpha error condition retains Taylor remainder, translation bias, numerical source error and amplification633, and must be strictly below 2^-82. Exact grid100 export changes representation only. Independent review passed 161286 checks.

The new comparison on the same twelve fixtures, author SHA96BB58D5, has been read but remains under independent review. Its length130 candidate has a projected core reserve of89268 events against the improved Horner schedule. Length260 still favors Horner on that subtotal. Neither observation supplies a family-wide decision or pays the remaining proof, conversion, cache and transport costs.

## Source-prime word streams

Accept the finite certificate design for complete prime-multiple progressions, sorted word grouping and exhaustive OR merges. A complete base-prime list through the exact square-root bound is essential. Positive-bit soundness suffices for the top bitmap; recursive base construction needs exact complement and extraction. Both merge tails must be consumed, including empty cases.

The reviewed operation model charges intermediate records, chunk/prime headers, table binding and transport. Empty streams still incur structural administration. Boolean gates are not MM0 commands or Lean reductions. Raw stream bytes do not bound parsed proof memory. The independent review passed 6232 checks and leaves ordinary-PA encodings, library proofs, actual source bits and coverage certificates open.

The next-hit carry successor is being researched against a stronger fresh quotient-witness header. It must pay for immutable state-vector identity and transport. The old division-circuit upper bound is not a valid comparator lower bound.

## Character transforms

Accept the matched fixed-grid arithmetic model and saved root/kernel split. In this specific circuit, each Bluestein kernel product corresponds to one final dyadic division, so G_kernel=D. The main scenario has88576420247296 kernel products and915527265950592 unit-root products. The model's independent review passed3749 checks.

The conditional p134 allocation has202 data magnitude bits,135 root bits and152 kernel bits. Its specified three-real-product cores cost7310 events per root product and8390 per kernel product. The resulting main arithmetic core is7674906601984255182 events. This is a conservative padded schedule at a candidate precision. It is not a verifier-command count, runtime measurement, actual stage-width distribution or proof that transforms dominate the complete cost.

The sample precisions112/134/152 correspond to separate intrinsic transform allowances2^-60/2^-82/2^-100 under the model's sufficient inequalities. They do not allocate the full sign budget. Input/source errors, scaling, phase, interpolation and spectral completeness remain separate obligations. Cached coefficient sums, kernel construction, proof bindings and memory must be charged.

The saved evidence lacks the current joint stage/type/height distribution. Root authorizes the bounded structural reconstruction described by integration's preflight: only conductor factorization, selected circuit choices, primitive-output restriction metadata, exact height/band counts and aggregate event classes. It may not enumerate unit residues or evaluate roots, characters, transforms, L-functions or zeros. Hard caps are one million context classes and64MiB per serialized artifact. All five operation categories must reproduce all four frozen scenarios, and setup remains separate. Conservative norm envelopes must not be described as actual widths.

## Active assignments after restoration

Integration reconstructs the bounded transform metadata; moebius independently reviews it. Independent-feasibility studies moving Bluestein normalization into the cached kernel, including the precision cost that may cancel a magnitude saving. Major-constants reviews moebius's static MM0 command-expansion report. Statement-audit reviews the same-fixture variable FD/Horner comparison. Prime-estimates authors the carry-header successor; zero-sum independently reviews it. Root integrates reviewed results and keeps complete-cost comparisons distinct from partial arithmetic cores.

Actual spectral acceptance, principal RH certification, finite binary coverage, the prime ladder, analytic libraries, ordinary-rule arithmetic implementations and resource calibration remain open. The compute-minimization goal stays active. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Stage widths, carry headers and checker expansion

2026-11-11. The preceding goal turn made progress by adopting four reviewed research packets and restarting bounded follow-up work. This turn adds a reviewed structural transform inventory, a reviewed improved fixture comparison, a reviewed sieve-header successor and a source-backed MM0 command model. Root read each author/review pair and verified26 associated report, script, receipt and manifest hashes. These are research results, not kernel theorems or measured resource savings.

## Transform widths can now be priced by stage

Accept integration's new structural export and moebius's independent review. The inventory preserves original axis order, primitive-output restrictions, all heights and the selected circuit. Its138126 event classes retain phase, local length, radix stage, conservative data/coefficients widths, division exponent and20 scenario-band multiplicities. Independent reconstruction passed2617127 checks, including the125400 conductor rows,35190 requests,220108 contexts, all five operation categories in all four scenarios and unchanged setup.

Operation multiplicities use preceding retained-output sizes. Magnitude propagation uses preceding full axis sizes. Confusing them would underestimate widths. Root coefficient exponents bound center norms, while data keys bound augmented norms. The same old safety/error conditions and an actual input bound remain prerequisites.

Root has authored a separate fold into the reviewed three/four-product arithmetic templates, keeping p112/134/152 and V<=2^14 unchanged. Its690718 checks reproduce all projections and old padded totals. At p134, its three-product arithmetic core is6305051715366094514 versus7674906601984255182 with the old uniform width. This is a17.84% to17.85% reduction in a specified subtotal, not a runtime result. The fold remains pending independent review at this checkpoint.

The scaled-kernel proposal is a separate circuit change. Its author derives a rounded K/P cache on the original grid with the old conservative local error/safety contract. It may remove repeated inverse divisions and reduce intermediate magnitudes, but changes centers and adds cache rounding and binding. That author and its context supplement remain under independent review. Any subsequent comparison must use the tighter original-circuit local-width schedule and reconstruct exact contexts from the manifest. Rounded old exponents cannot be inverted by subtracting log2(P).

## Same-fixture evaluator comparison

Accept the improved variable-degree/grid FD and Horner comparison, reviewed with1331 complete comparisons/assertions over509 FD and506 Horner schedules. Both sides use the same twelve saved cells and actual target denominator reductions. No unit scan was repeated. Short-cell normalization is now justified by the finite divisibility theorem with separate support bounds, and new beta errors are included in live/raw/output capacities.

For the length130 fixture, the chosen FD arithmetic core is436854 versus526122 for the improved fixed-shape Horner schedule. Its89268-event reserve must pay any net extra conversion, geometry, sign/range, cache, representation and transport costs. Length260 still favors Horner on the same subtotal by69376 events. These rows neither choose a global policy nor give a family-wide saving. Source generation and full twenty-slot delivery remain unchanged.

## Carrying the next sieve hit

Accept the complete carried-pointer invariant and matched fresh-header comparison. For every prime p and chunk start b, the pointer must be the least member of the progression beginning at(p^2-1)/2 that is at least b. Empty chunks preserve that pointer. An active transition checks n'=n+Hp and e<=n'<e+p. This proves both exhaustive hits in the current chunk and the successor invariant. Immutable list identity, prime order and exact chunk joins are required.

The independent review passed22111 checks and caught a preflight specification gap that the frozen author corrected. The fresh quotient-witness comparator must check its raw result's high-zero/packing proof before using narrow comparisons. Carry checks its successor inequalities at the raw width before narrowing. Neither may infer raw capacity from an unaccepted small result.

Against the specified fresh witness schedule, carrying removes5821/6471/7155 Boolean arithmetic gates per active or postactivation empty pair for source lengths2^32/2^34/2^36. Preactivation empties already have a cheap fresh branch and earn no such saving. Actual case counts require the actual complete base list. QP state reads, successor nodes and invariant steps, plus setup, packing, transport and proof work, remain charged. This is an accepted alternative construction, not a chosen implementation or net saving.

## MM0 arithmetic events need real proof templates

Accept the static source/counting report and its1359-check independent review. Under its specified first-use proof-DAG schedule, body commands including END equal H+R+C+2. H counts premise edges, R counts argument and conclusion expression requests, and C counts child requests of new expression constructors. The formula includes local sharing under exact expression identity. It excludes conversions, unfolding, statement boundaries, proof-library construction and actual invoked unification work.

Existing radix-16 examples give14/16 fresh-call commands and27/31 unshared header commands. Their hex coercion declarations remain additional axioms, so those source proofs do not establish a PA-only byte backend. The proposed202-event rectangular product and508-event signed-round example still require actual PA byte-cell/row/base and sign/range signatures and proof bodies. They are not202/508 verifier commands. The stock theorem-local heap/reset boundaries also prevent assuming free cache reuse across proof packs.

The next bounded arithmetic task is a source-backed paper product-cell lift, including its real premises, carry invariants and extra operations if the advertised five-event schedule cannot be realized. Prime-estimates authors it and moebius reviews. This is more useful for eventual estimates than attaching an arbitrary opcode multiplier to every arithmetic event.

## Remaining end-to-end work

The downstream spectral-budget audit is tracing exactly where current alpha already includes source and translation errors, and where intrinsic transform, finite-head, scaling, phase and interpolation errors enter. It must prevent double-counting and identify the missing actual margins needed to select precision. No numerical sign success is assumed.

Actual spectral certificates, principal RH certification, binary finite coverage, the prime ladder, analytic foundations, ordinary-rule implementations and calibration remain open. The goal remains active. No end-to-end core-hour estimate or unconditional QRH replacement is claimed.

# Local widths and cached normalization decisions

2026-11-11. This follows STAGE_WIDTHS_CARRY_AND_CHECKER_EXPANSION_DECISIONS_2026-11-11.md, SHA256DE7165A28FFE672EFEF9F14788D4E374F65263732452CD9BB88A48DEF6EB0CCC. Root has now read the completed independent reviews of its local-width fold and the normalized-cache mathematical proposal, including the context supplement. All13 report, script and receipt hashes in these two packets match. Accept the scoped results below. No production precision, successful spectral certificate or measured runtime saving is claimed.

## Accepted original-circuit comparison

The local-width fold passed695117 independent checks over all24 precision/product-model/scenario combinations and every band/component/capacity row. At unchanged p134 and the same V<=2^14 hypothesis, the three-product main arithmetic core is6305051715366094514 instead of7674906601984255182 under the old common allocation. The difference is1369854886618160668 events, between17.84% and17.85% of that padded baseline.

This gives a concrete improvement to a specified arithmetic schedule. Complete proof cost still includes local-width justification, numeral construction, signs, ranges, shifts, bindings, caches, transport and memory. The saving is conditional on their net extra cost fitting the weighted arithmetic reserve. It is not17.85% of the complete Goldbach proof. The other candidate precisions and extra-node scenarios remain separate alternatives.

The accepted fold preserves the existing circuit, computed centers, common grids and sufficient intrinsic-error conditions. Width allocation introduces no new numerical rounding. Root certification, cache construction and source/analytic errors remain. This tighter schedule is now the comparator for the normalized-kernel research.

## Accepted normalized-kernel mathematics

For a Bluestein leaf n>=2, let m=2n-1, P=2^k be its padded length, R=2k+4, A=1+3R and u=2^-p. Start with the existing certified kernel center Khat. The new cache rounds Khat/P onto grid p. Its error against exact K/P is at most(A+1)u, and the old local safety bound proves its center norm strictly below1.

The new point and inverse operations stay on grid p; the runtime inverse has no final division by P. The proof explicitly retains the factor P that amplifies point and inverse arithmetic errors. The resulting local normalized error coefficient C_new satisfies

    C_old - C_new = R(9P/2+n-10)-6 >=74.

Thus the old local error/safety pair remains sufficient at unchanged p. The old CT and product-axis error composition can consume the new local result. This is a conservative sufficient bound, not a claim that the actual numerical errors are unchanged or that the old bound was optimal.

Keeping exact Khat/P on grid p+k would retain its old integer numerator and supplies no automatic width saving. The newly rounded grid-p cache is the mathematical change that permits a p-bit kernel magnitude. It changes computed centers. Fresh sign and completeness evidence is necessary.

Under the additional inherited global semantic-error condition, the new inverse input has augmented factor Z_s=nm/P+2, and its sufficient local stored peak is2PZ_s=2nm+4P. The context supplement gives exact stage-indexed bounds and a2n final-chirp bound. Raw integer products and rounding witnesses still need their full pre-cancellation capacities. Independent review passed1693 exact checks and verified the general algebra separately from the finite examples.

At every runtime cache use, P complex final divisions disappear. Each actual cache construction adds P scaling rounds and must construct exact coefficient sums from the new rounded centers. The old kernel FFT producer remains. Under the existing one-precision/one-admission schedule, the main ledger has88576420247296 runtime divisions and337702368 cache entries. These occurrence counts do not establish the full price saving, especially if caches are duplicated or rebuilt.

The decision condition remains U*(R_old-R_new)>S_extra, using complete per-use and setup/lifetime prices for the chosen backend. Generation, proof production and checking need separate prices. No arithmetic acceptance rule or new permanent axiom is introduced.

## Next authorized work

Independent-feasibility is folding the normalized circuit from the already saved manifest, DAG and conductor records; major-constants independently reviews it. The preflight permits only bounded structural/rational metadata with at most one million contexts/classes and64MiB per output. It first reconstructs the original affected histogram and prices exactly, preserves unchanged phases and labels, then attaches the new exact context bounds. It does not factor or enumerate a new conductor family, scan units or evaluate roots/transforms/L-functions/zeros. Subtracting log2(P) from a rounded old exponent is not accepted.

The PA backend work now has a concrete product-cell author packet awaiting review. Its15 arguments and nine premises expose byte-range and carry obligations. Separate adder and row/base papers will determine whether the proposed arithmetic-event formulas admit a complete ordinary-PA realization. No paper signature is treated as an implemented theorem.

The complete spectral-budget audit and its independent review are also active. Actual finite coverage, principal RH certification, the prime ladder, analytic libraries, numerical certificates, ordinary implementations and calibration remain unresolved. The compute-minimization goal stays active; no final core-hour estimate is supplied.

# Normalized costs, complete errors and PA interfaces

2026-11-11. The previous goal turn made progress by adopting local transform widths and the normalized-cache mathematical proof. This turn accepts the reviewed normalized structural/cost fold, complete downstream error composition, Gaussian-window interpolation route, and explicit PA product/adder interfaces. Root read all author/review reports and verified13 report/clarification hashes. The source reports bind their independent metadata evidence. No compiled theorem, actual spectral success, selected precision or end-to-end runtime saving follows.

## A stronger transform comparison

The normalized-cache fold passed6223150 independent checks. It reconstructs all original affected event classes before replacement, preserves all other phases and labels, and prices all24 precision/product/scenario combinations. At unchanged p134, its main three-product core is6124737322160821530 against6305051715366094514 for the already improved original local-width circuit. The projected reduction is2.85% to2.86% of that subtotal.

The runtime reserve separates into151592179696483912 product events and28722213508789072 addition events. Cold additional cache setup costs51463736832 addition events in the same model. If the old sum cache is already built, the setup comparison instead charges77812805824. All remaining proof, representation, memory and lifecycle differences remain explicit prices. Kernel FFT setup stays. Kernel product counts remain positive even though runtime inverse-scale counts become zero; the old counting identity equating them must not be reused.

This accepted result is a complete comparison within the declared arithmetic subtotal. It is not a complete backend implementation or a reason to ignore principal, finite-range or analytic-library work.

## The complete error chain

The reviewed current alpha contract already includes Taylor truncation, finite translation bias, source numerical error and evaluator error after character scaling:

    A_alpha=T_K+S_shift+N_source+633*E_eval <2^-82.

It is charged once. For the actual character operator, stored transform Shat and conductor factor q=c^-s, a sufficient L radius is

    r_L=A_alpha+H_head+J_new+epsilon_T+M_S*e_q+e_scale.

H_head is accuracy of the finite head, not the radius-at-most-one used to bound magnitudes. J_new covers only new representation error. Intrinsic epsilon_T already includes root/chirp, kernel and transform-gate uncertainty under its theorem. The later root-number phase and completion factors are separate.

The p112/134/152 cases remain intrinsic scenarios for2^-60/2^-82/2^-100. They are not complete sign tolerances. In particular, allowing both alpha and transform errors2^-82 does not bound their sum by2^-82. Direct signs require an actual proxy center exceeding its complete radius. Interpolation additionally needs actual completed Y samples, kernel/tail bounds and a valid dependency set. Count closure remains its own strict inequality with certified critical-zero evidence, canonical phase integral and auxiliary weight.

The independent error-chain review passed43 exact/formal checks and inspected the actual consumer/source identities. Accept this composition as the common contract for further optimization. Actual margins and input/head/scaling/completion enclosures remain absent.

## An admitted interpolation route with explicit costs still needed

Platt section6 applies a Gaussian window to the endpoint-normalized Y used by the historical large branch. It does not specialize section5's strict-tilt theorem to eta=1. The independent review reconstructs the contour constants, Schwartz regularity and Fourier-periodization factor4.

The new all-real bound |Y(t)|<=144c(2+|t|) gives explicit two-sided sample tails for off-grid and low-height targets. With Delta5/64, h1, m256, c<=265800 and0<=t0<=7000, the Fourier and omitted-sample allowances are respectively below2^-46 and2^-243. This is a conditional analytic route, not a2^-82 enclosure or a guaranteed sign. Increasing m alone does not improve the Fourier allowance.

There are513 samples per direct target in this conservative instance. Negative samples require the correctly phased inverse-character channel multiplied by exp(-pi*u/2), with its own enclosure and arithmetic. Analytic compatibility of upper guards with height7000 does not mean the saved output schedule contains them.

Integration and zero-sum are constructing/reviewing an explicit saved-metadata guard plan. Manifest J is a sample count: N_last=J+extra-1, targets end at N_last*Delta, and the conservative positive count becomes J+extra+256. A shorter final evaluator admitted only through the old maximum cannot automatically be used for the guards. Use a declared-domain evaluator or prove a new complete alpha admission. The interpolation author/reviewer are also studying shorter windows at the same total analytic allowance; the m256 case remains the frozen baseline until that work is reviewed.

## PA arithmetic now has inspectable interfaces

Accept the original product cell with15 natural arguments and nine premises, together with its mandatory closed-fact clarification. Its five arithmetic events are four table equations and one fused lift. Ranges are genuine proof inputs. The182-command unshared header and26/46 cached/fresh call stencils are concrete source-format models. They are not emitted proofs or machine timings.

The reviewed row/base specification exports the complete low/high product invariant, including outgoing ranges, and uses a terminal specialization to conclude the original product equality. Two row roles and one initial base give the conditional5mn+2n+1 arithmetic core. Its actual connected-DAG command expression is25mn+22n+1+D+C, or1042+D+C for the13-by3 example. D counts only newly applied closed zero-argument/zero-premise facts. Parameterized range and binding producers require their full arguments, premises and terms.

The reviewed adder has an8-argument/2-premise equality lift, an optional8/7 range-checking lift and a1/0 terminal base. It retains the final carry. Its conditional command expressions are11l+D+C+4 and16l+D+C+4. No-carry removal, canonical numeral conversion and source/output bindings remain separate unless supplied by a proved specialized interface.

These paper results replace unspecified signatures with concrete proof obligations. They do not implement the PA tables, generic proof bodies or numeral adapters. New work targets those actual binding/term-growth costs, a literal-zero product-cell specialization and radix choices8/10/12/16 with table/format/cache costs. No native arithmetic acceptance rule or hex coercion axiom is adopted.

## Remaining objective

The goal remains minimizing end-to-end Lean/MM0 formalization compute. Actual spectral certificates, principal RH certification, binary finite coverage, the prime ladder, analytic foundations, ordinary-rule implementations and calibration remain unresolved. There is no basis to mark the objective complete or blocked, and no final core-hour estimate is supplied.

Decision SHA256: 7C4840E8E1E0A4A08783DC8E66C32E3430D22ECB2CEA48ADE4668F350D039D93


# Shorter windows, sample guards and PA radix decisions

2026-11-11. This follows the normalized-cost/error/interface decision, SHA256 7C4840E8E1E0A4A08783DC8E66C32E3430D22ECB2CEA48ADE4668F350D039D93. Root read the author and independent-review reports below and checked all twelve report hashes. Accept the results at their stated paper-proof and saved-metadata scopes. Their reviews bind the supporting scripts and receipts. Root did not rerun the writers or independent checkers in this turn. No compiled theorem, actual sign, complete zero count, selected production precision or end-to-end runtime result follows.

## Interpolation now has a shorter admitted candidate

Accept h=159/500, m=41, Delta=5/64 for the same endpoint-normalized Y and primitive nonprincipal family with conductor at most 265800. On the stated target domain, the analytic Fourier-plus-sample tail is below (927193523/1000000000)*2^-46. The independent review reconstructs the continuum bounds and checks the finite rational certificate, including a separate direct Taylor certificate. This improves the previous analytic allowance of 2^-46+2^-243.

The direct window uses 83 samples and 82 accumulation additions instead of 513 and 512. It removes 430 products and 430 additions per query in that particular finite-sum schedule. This is not a full error bound or a global query-cost result. The Gaussian kernel changes with h. Its actual enclosure, arithmetic, cache and proof costs remain required. The number of queries and repair attempts is unknown. Minimality of m=41 holds only for the declared sufficient test on its finite grid.

The source proof uses the all-real growth envelope 1512*(2+|t|), valid for the specified family. It retains the exact normalization, negative-channel identity and off-grid tail argument. It does not invoke the strict-tilt theorem at its excluded endpoint. All analytic formalization leaves remain unimplemented.

## Both guard schedules are reconciled, and the shorter one is preferred for this comparison

Accept the reviewed m256 ledger as the frozen comparator and the reviewed m41 successor as the new conditional schedule. For sample count J and auxiliary choice e, the target interval still ends at (J+e-1)*Delta. The m41 support requires exactly 41 new positive indices, J+e through J+e+40. A real-distance bound of 42*Delta does not require a 42nd sample. Targets do not expand to the new guard endpoint.

The independent successor review checks all 125400 saved conductor rows, all four alternative scenarios, all five bands and the direct deleted interval. On identical source, full P20 evaluator and original-transform assumptions, the new schedule removes 215 guard heights per conductor. Per scenario this removes:

|Quantity|Removed instances|
|---|---:|
|General complex products|26410857718350|
|Complex additions/subtractions|54713575830105|
|Full P20 residue Horner stages|29138367222720|
|Positive primitive outputs requiring completion|931648071245|
|Shared final-band head reciprocal scales|35674627500|

The complete compared schedules are O_e+256V and O_e+41V, with identical original O_e. The reductions above are not the whole campaign. Band weights are recomputed by interval intersection; they are not uniformly rescaled. Normalized Bluestein costs and newer shorter evaluators are not silently substituted into this original-circuit comparison.

The maximum required positive guard height becomes 26525/4=6631.25, below the declared source cap 7000. It still exceeds the older actual-height cap 424195/64. Consequently the old K18/b94 admission cannot automatically be used for these new samples. Retain the declared-domain source and matched full P20 baseline, or prove a new complete evaluator admission.

Negative samples use the inverse character and the exact fixed phase sign, multiplied by exp(-pi*u/2). They do not copy the same character at positive height. The optional full negative adapter now needs 41 shared attenuation enclosures and 177663120563 scalar transports. Existing positive inverse indices are in the required schedule, but their completed values have not been produced. Zero is retained once; central multiplicities and nonreal two-channel conventions are unchanged.

The source and transform setup dimensions remain unchanged. Existing setup, cache reconstruction, positive completion, canonical phases, new kernels and actual certificate checking remain paid. These results justify preferring the shorter matched guard schedule for further pricing; they do not establish a complete runtime winner.

## The PA cell has a smaller ordinary-rule equality interface

Accept the zero-carry specialization. Its fourth table premise is literally (u+e)+f=beta*0+k. Together with the preceding three equations and the higher-tail equation, this proves the unchanged cell conclusion by natural semiring identities. The implication drops the overflow variable and four local range premises. Completeness for valid byte inputs still uses their ranges and canonical table outputs to establish that the required fourth entry has zero carry.

The lift now has 14 expression arguments and five premises. Its unshared header has 134 commands instead of 182; cached/fresh calls have 21/41 instead of 26/46. The conditional row command core becomes 20mn+22n+1+D+C rather than 25mn+22n+1+D+C. For the 13-by-3 example the constants are 847 and 1042. The five-event cell count itself is unchanged.

The complete arithmetic table is still 3*256^2 facts. Source and intermediate digit ranges, carry binding, numeral conversions, row invariants and public product transport remain genuine proof work. D counts only newly applied closed zero-argument, zero-premise facts. No native arithmetic acceptance or hex coercion axiom is introduced.

Under matched literal terms, tables and producers, the shorter lift removes one argument request and four premise references per call without requiring a new conversion. This is a local checking improvement. It does not prove equal global constructor costs or permit deletion of range producers needed elsewhere.

## Larger radices require a complete representation comparison

Accept the general-radix report as a sensitivity comparison using the older full cell and the same saved full20 Horner capacities. It does not incorporate the zero-carry specialization. For the first-four fixed-profile example, product-template events at w=8,10,12,16 are 10702,5920,5040,3980. These counts alone do not select an efficient implementation. Header execution, definitions, table proof generation, numeral binding, memory and actual cache lifetimes remain part of the comparison.

The complete table has 3*2^(2w) arithmetic facts. A full w16 table exceeds the stock u32 theorem-count field, including the stated symmetric-table alternative. The w12 obstruction is narrower: the specified disjoint expanded binary-numeral headers cannot all satisfy the stock signature-start address bound. This is not a universal four-GiB file limit or a prohibition on radix4096. Conservative named-digit definitions, shared or sparse tables and changes to the format that preserve the proof rules require their own accounting.

Independent checking reconstructs all 912 capacity pairs and their saved aggregate scenarios. Actual digit diversity and cache hits are not inferred from bit capacities. No time comparison follows from subtracting two upper header bounds. Setup generation, repeated library checking and imports must be separated when their lifetimes differ.

## A complete conditional radius admits the p112 intrinsic term

Accept the independently reviewed stability allocation. The exact kernel absolute-sum is below 5. The actual completion multiplier has magnitude below 27, using the maximum of the two separate Gamma bounds, and |L|<4032. The final stored transform center is below 2^21 by a noncircular comparison using the exact conductor factor before approximating it. This final-center bound does not replace the internal FFT magnitude bounds.

Full alpha error below 2^-82, intrinsic transform error at most 2^-60 and the explicitly stated head, representation and scale tolerances give rL<2^-59. The actual completion requires canonical phase and amplitude enclosures; the coarse sign proxy cannot replace it. The direct sample radius is below 2^-52. The paid inverse-character adapter yields a common sample radius at most 2^-51 and stored magnitude below 2^17.

With 83 kernel radii at most 2^-72 and total accumulation error at most 2^-51, the exact split K*(Y-Yhat)+(K-Khat)*Yhat gives

    rI < 2^-46 + 5*2^-51 + 83*2^17*2^-72 + 2^-51
       = (691/1024)*2^-45 < (7/10)*2^-45.

The independent review checks the ordered error and magnitude arguments, including the positive-sample bound before negative transport. The p112 intrinsic transform case satisfies its allocated 2^-60 term under its existing input, root/kernel and safety hypotheses. This makes p112 a compatible candidate for this complete output-radius contract. It does not select it for production or establish useful signs, successful brackets or completeness. All listed producer tolerances still need actual certificates and costs. The source alpha contract is not loosened.

## Work continuing after this checkpoint

The numeral-binding author supplies explicit concatenation, padding and public binary-source adapters. Root has read it and assigned independent review. Its padded digit representation is not automatically interchangeable with the named-digit table proposal. That conversion remains charged. The named-definition author/reviewer and streamed-proof research continue separately.

The next analytic research task is a repair policy distinguishing failures that increased arithmetic precision can fix from failures requiring a different window, points or zero evidence. Actual spectral certificates, principal RH verification, finite binary coverage, the prime ladder, analytic library construction and backend calibration remain unresolved. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

Decision SHA256: C620596355D2FE17F921BD4637281F478208CFE1930D26527DC3CE6C59FC5E65


# Numeral bindings, named digits and precision repairs

2026-11-11. The previous goal turn made progress by adopting the reviewed shorter window, guard schedules and complete stability allocation in decision C620596355D2FE17F921BD4637281F478208CFE1930D26527DC3CE6C59FC5E65. This turn accepts the independently reviewed numeral-binding, named-digit, repair and lower-precision results below. Root inspected all eight reports, including its own lower-precision author packet, and checked their hashes. Their reviews bind the separate source and static evidence. No compiled theorem, actual spectral certificate, full stock-checker admission or runtime saving is established.

## Public numeral equality now has explicit proof producers

Accept the proposed PA product numeral-binding design. Literal next-state terms supply the three intermediate prefix/place bindings through actual reflexivity applications. Two passes of a fixed concatenation lemma bind the row calculation to the supplied multiplier and final product word. The padded inner base retains the high carry in exactly the word form consumed by the next row. A one-time zero-word proof supplies the initial carry state.

The final transport consumes equalities for the original public operands and output and returns exactly x_src*Y_src=Z_src. Binary-source conversion handles terminal one, high-zero padding, byte grouping and equality orientation. It does not recompute the large product. The positive nonempty branch retains its actual positivity proof; zero operands use their separate branch.

The reviewed caller schedule outside the substituted arithmetic core has 5n+m+4 new theorem applications and 20n+3m+26 premise/expression-root requests. A deliberately loose complete binding/positive-source bound is 4mn+1313m+1324n+668 requests including the stated constructor reserves. It applies only to the declared binary grammar, lengths and shared-object schedule. The padded base adds twelve fresh-conclusion child requests per row compared with the former base; substitute this once or add 12n, not both.

These costs are additional to actual arithmetic, range/table proofs and generic library construction. The independent review reconstructs 22 signatures and 4059 static checks. It does not establish memory fit or supply emitted proof streams. Keeping source bindings explicit prevents the older arithmetic-only count from being mistaken for the full certificate cost.

## Named digits remove one specific format obstruction

Accept the conservative named-natural design and its independent review. Existing d0/d1 remain literal; the other digit names and radix are checked nullary definitions with bodies using earlier ordinary natural terms. Closed product/adder headers then have ten/twelve commands. UTerm matching does not silently unfold a name. Def checking, explicit Conv/Unfold work and exact local object sharing remain required.

For w12 the complete arithmetic table still has 50331648 facts. Under the specified three-byte new-head layout, theorem entries plus headers occupy between 1644118016 and 1711226880 bytes. A broader all-five-byte-term bound with seven alignment bytes per header is 3405774848 bytes. Definition/prelude data and other required libraries are additional. These bounds remove the old disjoint expanded-header obstruction conditionally, without proving full file, name-index, proof-stream or local-store admission. The complete w16 theorem-count obstruction remains.

The review clarifies that the metadata field 104*beta^2 permits five-byte NEW heads while retaining two-byte OLD heads. It is not the all-term-five-byte bound. The latter is separately priced as 182*beta^2 before the stated alignment reserve. These different encodings must not be conflated.

The named generic library needs its own proved specialization to the named radix. The 20-command setup conversion schema excludes original PA proof generation, missing saved-body construction and executed definition headers. Comparing a named wider radix against an unnecessarily expanded byte implementation would be unfair: both can use named digits.

The numeral-binding design produces fixed-width digit bodies ending in zero. The named-digit packet uses canonical binary bodies. The two reviewed designs are therefore not yet a single free composition. A proved conversion or a separately reviewed compatible definition-body design remains necessary. The proposed follow-up using fixed-width bodies for nonzero/nonone aliases is research, not an adopted change.

## Repair decisions follow the failed certificate inequality

Accept the reviewed precision-repair policy. Producer-contract failure, failure of an actual strict sign and failure of count closure are separate events. Increasing transform p changes only its allocated error contribution unless the other producers are also refined. At fixed other tolerances its sufficient output radius is R_fixed+135*C_T*2^-p. This bound cannot meet a target below its retained R_fixed by changing p alone. R_fixed is an upper allowance, not a lower bound on actual error.

Changing m reduces the omitted-sample allowance without reducing the Fourier allowance. Changing h affects both tails and kernel values. A genuinely finer coarse lattice changes Nyquist and the sample/guard costs; merely refining target offsets on the old lattice does not. Every new target set requires its exact dependency union and admitted source domains. Direct point evaluation can avoid this interpolation tail under its own complete contract.

Every retained sign must pass its actual |center|>radius test. When a true-value margin mu is separately known, 2r<mu is sufficient; r<mu alone is not. This corrects any weaker interpretation of older informal margin wording. At a true zero the strict test cannot succeed. Opposite signs establish a critical lower count, not exact multiplicity, and even multiplicity can be missed by signs. Central and inverse-channel conventions remain unchanged.

Finite-RH closure retains the strict pair-deficit inequality h_T*(K+2)-Abar+W-2*Bbar>0 with its actual critical evidence and canonical integral. It is not an exact zero-list claim. More value bits help only when they produce useful admissible evidence. Generation pays failed attempts; checking may retain the successful dependency union, with exact bindings, reusable objects and cache lifetimes accounted for. No repair frequency or unconditional termination is assumed.

## Lower precision preserves the complete error ceiling

Accept the separately frozen root allocation EEBC987DD386E2ED50CC7F56C82714FC1EF51C8A3DF22C2579FFC108C0C1BD39 after zero_sum's independent review and 216 exact checks. The author produced 170 scalar checks; these are mathematical metadata, not function values. The proof retains all non-transform tolerances and re-establishes the ordered magnitude bounds for p>=100. It replaces the old intermediate sample-radius allocation when lowering p.

The sufficient sample radius is 27*(C_T*2^-p+2^-82+4*2^-63)+2*2^-54+2*2^-55. The exact-kernel split, 83 kernel errors, accumulation and sharper accepted analytic tail give p108 a complete radius below 0.653052762*2^-45. This is stronger than the previous 691/1024 ceiling. A separate p106 candidate is below 0.837311263*2^-45. The intrinsic theorem and normalized-cache proof are parameterized, so the earlier p112 example is not a lower validity threshold. All safety, grid100 embedding, stored-input and cache hypotheses remain required.

The p108 candidate spends unused analytic and intrinsic-error reserve while preserving the source/head/completion/kernel tolerances. It does not inherit numerical centers or successful signs. Threshold minimality concerns only the displayed sufficient scalar tests. Production precision remains unselected. A separate matched saved-width cost fold is under independent review; its subtotal must retain actual setup and cache lifetimes before any cost decision.

## Work continuing after this checkpoint

Workers are also examining checked local-definition/theorem admission, streamed multiplication joins and reusable dyadic-offset kernel certificates. These new packets require their own review and adoption. Actual signs, spectral completeness, principal certification, finite binary coverage, prime ladders, analytic libraries and backend calibration remain open. The active objective remains minimizing the complete proof's expected generation and verification cost, and no final core-hour estimate is supplied.

Decision SHA256: 9D627C560AB0151C92F10526753E32C36E8F8F638393DB9883EDC5BF290A6304


# Matched costs for the lower-precision candidates

2026-11-11. Supplement to decision 9D627C560AB0151C92F10526753E32C36E8F8F638393DB9883EDC5BF290A6304, which accepted the independently reviewed p106/p108 complete-error allocations. Root read the full cost author and independent review and verified their report pins. Accept the comparison at its declared arithmetic-template and saved-metadata scope. No production precision, actual sign or runtime winner is selected.

The independent reviewer reconstructed all 125279 saved event classes, 133 width classes, twenty scenario/band weights, 6004 saved kernel lengths and fourteen setup classes. All sixteen older p112/p134 cases match in every band and component before the lower-precision cases are compared. All 3486238 independent metadata checks passed. No new circuit, conductor inventory or numerical value was produced.

|Precision|Main three-product arithmetic core|
|---:|---:|
|106|4302622878721057430|
|108|4369084390425909710|
|112|4752826652495743914|
|134|6124737322160821530|

The p108 subtotal is between 8.07% and 8.08% below p112. This is a reduction in the specified product/addition event core, not the entire transform producer/checker or Goldbach proof. The underlying byte event template remains unchanged. Named digits, wider radices, the smaller zero-carry lift and new public-numeral adapters are not silently folded into these numbers. The four auxiliary-prefix scenarios and three/four-real-product alternatives remain separate.

The p108-versus-p112 main runtime reserve is 366515505176327696 product-template events plus 17226756893506508 addition-template events. When each side constructs one new compatible normalized cache, setup saves another 3893386112 addition-template events. If the p112 cache is already paid and reusable, the p108 candidate instead pays its full new setup subtotal of 64162618688 addition-template events. An old cache is not automatically reusable at a new precision.

Root/chirp/kernel FFT setup counts remain, including 337702368 kernel entries. Their width-sensitive generation and checking costs need not stay equal when their counts stay equal. With product/addition prices alpha,beta and remaining costs U, the cold comparison favors p108 only if

    U_108-U_112 < alpha*366515505176327696
                 + beta*(17226756893506508+3893386112).

Actual width-dependent prices require the saved class-level functional instead of two common weights. U retains signed/range/rounding/source-binding proofs, elementary and kernel production, cache imports/rebuilding, all unaffected numerical work, repair attempts and analytic foundations. Separate generation and verification functionals remain required.

The accepted lower-precision mathematics gives p108 a complete m41 radius below 0.653052762*2^-45 under every stated source, input, root/kernel, scaling, completion and finite-sum tolerance. That is stronger than the previous 691/1024 ceiling. The count comparison therefore has a consistent conditional mathematical basis. It does not establish actual certificates or preserve old numerical signs. The newly added m41 guard heights also require their own compatible inventory/evaluator pricing; they are not present merely because the original saved scenario was repriced here.

This result makes p108 the leading lower-precision candidate for the existing complete-radius target in further comparisons. p106 remains a separate looser-radius option. The compatible end-to-end configuration and actual success-dependent repair costs remain under research. No core-hour estimate is supplied.

Decision SHA256: D08A3DEB0C98207E0DA4370760AB12BCBD68B86EB18AAF5692F664384D78CFC2


# Guard-domain admission, kernel reuse and proof boundaries

2026-11-11. The previous goal turn made progress through the reviewed lower-precision mathematics and matched cost fold, culminating in decision D08A3DEB0C98207E0DA4370760AB12BCBD68B86EB18AAF5692F664384D78CFC2. This turn accepts the four independently reviewed results below at their conditional paper/source scopes. Root read all eight reports, including its own guard-domain author note, and verified their hashes. No numerical certificates, compiled theorems, implemented streaming checker or whole-file admission are claimed.

## The shortened Horner candidates cover the added guards

Accept the fresh alpha-budget proof at maximum guard height 26525/4=6631.25. The fixed-profile K18,b0=94 candidate has complete alpha error below 0.861409838*2^-82; K19,b0=93 is below 0.939320968*2^-82. Both meet the retained 2^-82 allocation. The largest guard index is 84880, exactly 41 beyond the old common maximum 84839. Only the largest auxiliary scenario exceeds that former common cap; the unified new proof covers all four scenarios.

The Taylor target term uses the new cap and D_out8192. Source magnitude, finite translation and coefficient allowances still use the declared 7000 source recipe and D_source4096. The full source delivers twenty centers with its existing P28/m159 work. K18 is not admitted throughout 7000 by this result, and shorter scalar consumption does not remove source slots or translations.

For each unchanged candidate, the coefficient and accumulator envelopes are identical to those previously reviewed. K18/94 therefore retains its 8932 product/2420 addition template subtotal per final-band residue evaluation; K19/93 retains 8858/2448. The reserve 74alpha-28beta remains conditional on all changed conversion, sign/range, alignment and representation costs. Exact grid100 exports and later p>=100 embeddings remain paid bindings. New numerical centers need their own strict-sign checks.

This removes a domain incompatibility in the candidate plan. It does not provide a joint normalized-transform guard inventory or select one Horner candidate. Individual guard weights still use their actual source bands, and the first four bands retain their own admitted evaluators.

## Kernel certificates can be shared with a stable error allocation

Accept the dyadic kernel schedule for h159/500, m41 and Delta5/64. The kernel depends on the exact distance d-theta, not the conductor, character or integer target index. Evenness permits absolute-distance keys while retaining the sinc sign. Complementary offsets reflect the stencil to [-40,42], so reversing an unchanged 83-entry array would miss a boundary coefficient.

For actual requested nonzero offset classes Theta, set F=Theta union (1-Theta). The exact noninteger distance-key count is D=41|F|+|Theta|. For a complete dyadic level with Q=2^R, this is 42(Q-1), cumulatively covering earlier levels after canonical reduction. These are conditional set counts, not an assumed query population. At theta=0 the kernel is exactly one at zero and zero at the other integer offsets; a grid query can use its certified sample with the required binding.

For u=l+f and t=min(f,1-f), the factorization

    K(u)=(-1)^l*(t/u)*sinc(pi*t)*exp(-A*u^2),
    A=390625/12943872,

has t/u<=1. It avoids amplifying a shared sine error by a small dyadic denominator. The ratio is exact in the specified final multiply-round stage; approximating it first would require another error term.

Two fractional Gaussian seed roles per f plus 41 shared integer-Gaussian roles support the recurrence. Clamped seed and gate centers on grid80 with errors at most 2^-80 give Gaussian error at most 85*2^-80 and final signed kernel error at most 88*2^-80<2^-72. Actual elementary, projection, multiplication, rational-round and sign proofs remain necessary. The sinc truncation estimate alone is not a complete seed enclosure.

The schedule pays 3D-2|F| general products and their rounds, D exact rational stages and 4D-2|F| post-gate projections, plus seed producers, source/key bindings and cache lifetimes. It retains the existing 83 products and 82 additions per direct nonlattice query. Cold sparse demand can make this seed-sharing schedule less attractive; no runtime saving follows from its role count alone. Its rK contract is compatible with the accepted p108 allocation because that kernel tolerance is unchanged.

## Stock MM0 can use checked private arithmetic facts

Accept the source/layout audit of the named w12 design. The complete 50331648-fact table cannot have all its names in the stock public theorem trie. LocalDef and LocalThm avoid public-name insertion while retaining ordinary definition/proof checking and global ID availability in the same checked stream. Local visibility does not mean unchecked, short-lived or free to apply.

The conservative named metadata reservation leaves 888631192 bytes below the largest u32 start for all remaining prefix data. This is conditional capacity, not a concrete layout or full admission. Ordinary arithmetic/range/decoder proofs, exact dependency order, public transcript, offsets, individual proof displacements, local memory limits and the selected parser-enabled executable still need validation. The final public statement must eliminate inaccessible private heads or use deliberately public source definitions through actual checked conversions.

The definition/theorem descriptors survive declaration boundaries; local head, body and proof pointers do not. Fresh files need real prerequisite closure. Neither a producer digest nor a previous result log is a theorem import. The complete w16 theorem-count obstruction remains separate.

## Product chunking has an explicit ordinary-rule boundary

Accept the proposed local-word export

    A_in+x*Y_block=D_block+P_block*A_out.

Closed chunks prove this equation from their supplied digit/carry witnesses. Adjacent carry words must match or have a proved equality; the first carry is proved zero, and the final high carry remains in the output. The new nine-argument/two-premise row lift and thirteen-argument/five-premise join are ordinary algebra. Exact append/place proofs discharge their bindings, then the reviewed public-numeral transport restores x_src*Y_src=Z_src.

The inspected leaf model still uses the original full cell. Its non-table-instantiation request subtotal is 25mr+15r+3. A join of lengths r,s adds 14r+5s+29 before constructor/header/import work; root append/extraction adds 7n+13. These are not complete proof or runtime costs and do not already include the zero-carry specialization.

Balanced joins yield O(n log K+Km) boundary syntax/work for K chunks while retaining all mn cell positions. Stock declaration boundaries release local scratch but retain accumulating global metadata. A bounded live theorem frontier is a proposed continuing-checker implementation, not existing stock behavior. Whole-file mapping is also distinct from simultaneous resident memory.

Apply the independent review's mandatory taint qualification: in the non-BARE development checker, internal counters can advance before a final Sorry rejection. Counter advancement alone therefore cannot authorize external theorem publication. A clean ordinary stream or an explicit no-taint commit rule is required, along with completion of pending conversion obligations. This qualifies the author's publication wording without changing its frozen algebraic interfaces.

Compare clean Lean dependency builds and imports with a complete ordinary MM0 check, or complete independent rechecks on both sides. Warm trusted imports and fresh repeated library replay are different operational workloads. No language-speed factor or new native arithmetic acceptance rule is adopted.

## Remaining integration work

The joint normalized-transform +41 histogram is being derived from the same saved request contexts, with exact old original and normalized histogram reconstruction required before crediting new guards. It will keep source, residue evaluation, negative transport, completion and query costs separate. The present papers do not supply that combined total.

Padded named-digit compatibility, the single-candidate audit and other new reports require separate adoption. Conductor/plan-specific precision is now being investigated from the actual error-composition theorem and saved schema before any new bounded fold. Principal certification, small conductors, finite binary coverage, prime ladders, actual signs/count closure, analytic libraries and backend calibration remain unresolved. No final core-hour estimate is supplied.

Decision SHA256: 20F3B32758EBC24C9DE95E33268386F04FCE016CB76AD03D5AE38FE618BBFE8D


# Guarded costs, compatible representations and local precision

2026-11-11. Root accepts the following independently reviewed research at its stated paper, source and saved-metadata scope. All eleven report hashes below were checked. This records progress beyond decision20F3B327: the normalized guard-cost join is now available, and a conductor-local precision rule has a reviewed complete-error contract. No actual spectral certificates, compiled declarations, arithmetic library, end-to-end runtime saving or standard-axioms closure is claimed.

## Normalized guard costs are joined

Accept the normalized-transform +41 guard inventory and independent review. Both complete predecessor histograms and the new guard histogram reconstruct from the saved contexts. The review checks all bands, four alternative prefixes, three precisions, two arithmetic modes and unchanged setup dimensions. At p108 in three-product mode, the added guard product-plus-addition template subtotal is21951764813119114. Main joined subtotal is4391036155239028824; the e640 alternative is4733697849882839384. These are arithmetic-template event sums, not machine instructions, seconds or complete proof costs. The alternatives are not added together.

Full P20 source/evaluation guard assumptions remain the baseline in this frozen packet. The subsequently admitted shortened Horner schedules require their own joined residue/conversion calculation. Root has approved that separate bounded saved-record calculation, with all five matched K20/b94 admissions required and original-operand conversion costs retained. The common full-grid source schedule adds41 final global layers, while conductor-specific residue costs retain their actual five-band guard weights. Kernel products remain present; normalized runtime divisions are zero. Negative adapters, positive completion, source production, query kernels/counts and other work remain separate.

## Compatibility audit requires its principal correction

Accept the one-candidate compatibility audit only together with its mandatory principal-scope clarification and independent review. The selected principal requirement is RH through10^8 with the chosen producer's actual support/count, endpoint and analytic prerequisites. There is no independent selected consumer requirement N(1500)=1069 or Q1500<1.668. Those are charged only if used by the chosen producer or an explicitly selected alternative route. Principal certification remains unpriced, nonzero work.

Two gaps described in that older audit now have successor evidence. The normalized +41 cost association is supplied above, and the K18/K19 guard-domain admission was accepted in decision20F3B327. Neither successor supplies actual values, successful strict signs or Turing completeness. The older audit's expanded-byte reference is not silently converted into a named-digit implementation.

## Padded named digits have a compatible source interface

Accept the padded-name successor as a paper representation design. For digits other than zero and one, the alias body is exactly the source decoder's fixed-width bit string. Literal zero and one require ordinary PA equalities, not definitional reduction. The five-argument, three-premise glue consumes the actual digit equality and returns the named-radix word. The public source product conclusion and all high carries remain unchanged.

At w12 this representation has53235 definition-body nodes, compared with45069 in the canonical-name proposal. Compact table-header shapes remain10/12 commands only after the matched tables and library have ordinary proofs. Canonical-spelling proofs need paid spelling conversions. Closed bridge facts, source construction, pointer identity, local lifetimes, range proofs, imports and packing remain explicit costs. This closes a paper representation mismatch; no stock checker admission or universal speed advantage is inferred.

## Gate comparison gives a useful negative result

Accept the fixed/Gauss/four comparison at matched exact operands, p, rounding and cache identity. The three algebraic schedules preserve centers at the same precision when all intermediate operations are exact and the final rounding rule is identical. This does not transfer signs across precision changes.

At p106 and p108, fixed-coefficient multiplication wins every saved product-width class under the declared unit-priced byte templates. Cached Gauss has the same product subtotal and additional addition work. No improvement for the leading p108 candidate is credited. At p112, kernel-only Gauss has a conditional main reserve of8039890902776512 product events against5788010916952704 added addition events. Root classes remain fixed. Cache unions, source bindings and full proof costs can change the comparison, so this is not a production selection or runtime result.

## Conductor-local precision is mathematically admitted for a bounded first pass

Accept the reviewed sufficient rule with epsilon_c(p)=2*phi(c)*S(c)*2^14*2^-p/sqrt(c), using one p for every character and height of conductor c. For p100 through108, test exactly

    (2*phi(c)*S(c))^2 * 2^(2*(108-p)) <= c*(1266*202305920)^2.

Retain the old safety/peak guards, grid100 input contract and every non-transform tolerance. The selector preserves the stronger complete global-p108 radius, whose ratio to2^-45 is2804840254168152529/4294967296000000000. Repeated axes remain repeated summands. It is a sufficient selector, not an optimum for actual error or full cost.

Root authorizes the first pass exactly as preflighted: the two pinned saved inputs,125400 rows, at most nine scalar tests per row, and at most334053 full-plan/precision states. No guard or price pass is implicit. Cache keys include the fixed u/enclosure proof contract, normalization, rounding and lifetime. Distinct precision caches are charged; finer existing caches are not freely reused. The distribution and duplication cost remain unknown at this decision. A separate bounded price preflight is being prepared before any propagation of local precision through runtime contexts.

All seven restored workers have bounded assignments: two author/reviewer pairs for local precision and guarded residue costs, an author/reviewer pair for a sharper normalized error recurrence, and one price-pass preflight. Root performs integration and scope control. No new numerical evaluations, builds, declarations, proof emission or unbounded inventory generation are authorized by this decision.

Decision SHA256: 972884F678C29412D66449B8BA961AFB6A3D77CDF8D2CBF151A5F3C79D88F1A6


# Local precision evidence and shared kernel construction

2026-11-11. The previous goal turn made progress through decision972884F678C29412D66449B8BA961AFB6A3D77CDF8D2CBF151A5F3C79D88F1A6 and the first saved precision pass. This turn advances the research by accepting its independent reconstruction, a sharper symbolic error recurrence, and a newly derived shared-high-precision kernel adapter. Root read all six complete reports and checked their hashes. These are saved-metadata and conditional mathematical results, not formal theorem closure or measured runtime savings.

## The first precision assignment is independently reconstructed

Accept the first conductor-local saved pass and its review. All125400 conductor rows, their ordered repeated axes, safety and budget slacks, and least admitted precision match an independent reconstruction. The reviewer rebuilt the old mixed recurrence from the saved plan and used a distinct bit-length selector and descending precision-mask cache propagation.

The p100 through108 conductor counts are106625,4985,4084,3503,2472,1996,807,920,8. These are not operation weights. The full-plan cache closure has38147 node/precision states and7606 kernel keys, with364355904 entries. Relative to the common-precision6004-key337702368-entry cache, it adds1602 keys and26653536 entries. Both source files explicitly exclude guards and prices. The recorded per-p producer identity and fixed enclosure contract are preserved.

The next required quantitative step is a matched cost propagation through actual conductor/DAG norm contexts. It must reconstruct the accepted globalp108 comparator and include actual +41 guard weights under a separately reviewed preflight. The largest row population alone cannot establish the largest cost share. No percent runtime saving follows from this pass.

## A sharper error recurrence is available without weakening guards

Accept the normalized Bluestein leaf coefficient

    C_sharp=9PA/2+3P/2+3Rn+12R+14,

with the old leaf D. Ordered CT composition uses C_m+C_r+2 and the same oriented safety expression C_m+C_r(2C_m+1). Same-plan structural induction gives C_sharp<=C_mix and D_sharp<=D_mix. This is not a general comparison of mixed-plan D with whole-axis baseline D.

The conductor selector may replace S_mix with the sum of these sharper axis coefficients, preserving repeated axes. It retains all old safety and peak guards, grid100, input/source contracts and every downstream tolerance. Its least sufficient p cannot increase, but no new distribution has been computed or credited. The complete common radius remains exactly the same p108 upper ceiling. Smaller error C is not itself a smaller magnitude bound or an ordinary-proof saving.

A successor saved pass requires new identified C/S fields and output artifacts. The frozen old new_C field remains the earlier mixed coefficient. A new price comparison must consume the actual successor assignment and its cache set. Its preflight is being prepared separately.

## One high kernel can supply multiple precision caches

Accept root's shared-high producer adapter together with the independent review. For one exact kernel identity and a set of required precisions, choose H=max p. A full raw kernel certificate at H has error PA*2^-H. Directly rounding Khat_H/P to p gives error at most A*2^-H+2^-p, hence at most(A+1)*2^-p. Safety at p implies safety at H.

Alternatively construct the normalized high cache B_H once. Keep it unchanged for p=H, and project directly from B_H for each p<H. Integer precision gives2^-H<=2^-p/2 and A>=1, so the bound(A+1)*2^-H+2^-p is still at most(A+1)*2^-p. Arbitrary chained rounding is not admitted. The two schedules need not produce the same centers.

The exact-kernel enclosure together with P(A+1)<Q supplies the strict low center norm below1. Thus the existing normalized runtime error and magnitude proofs consume the same numerical hypotheses. This is a successor producer identity, not a relabeling of the frozen independent-p cache objects. New centers need their own strict-sign checks. Runtime root/chirp certificates remain separate.

For each actual lifetime, let E_all=sum|T_n|P_n and E_high=sum P_n. Both schedules retain E_all runtime entries and2E_all exact real coefficient sums. The direct-raw schedule has E_all raw scaling projections. The normalized-high schedule has E_high high scaling projections and E_all-E_high low projections. One high full kernel construction per exact identity replaces separate per-p construction roles, subject to actual proof reuse. No setup runtime is credited yet.

Original numerator widths and shifts remain paid. A raw high projection starts on gridH+log2P; a normalized-high projection starts from an H-bit magnitude numerator. Cached sums must be exact sums of the new rounded coordinates. High sums cannot be independently rounded and silently reused. Source bindings, ordinary projection proofs, cache lifetimes, stock imports/replays, retention and storage remain necessary. Cold comparisons and already-sunk setup have different costs.

## Current work and remaining objective

The guarded residue frontier has completed its author pass and is under independent review. The matched conductor-specific price preflight and sharper-assignment preflight are being reviewed before execution. Further paper work examines precision portfolios and high-precision root/chirp projections. None changes the conservative baseline without its own evidence.

No numerical source/kernel/root certificates, strict signs, Turing completeness, principal computation, small-family closure, finite binary/prime-ladder certificate or ordinary arithmetic library has been supplied by these tasks. No end-to-end Lean/MM0 Fermi estimate is issued in this decision. The goal remains active.

Decision SHA256: 05EE39C563E731694321B98FF4049C14C8C3C640A27809B62B02E8C23044C3E7


# Guarded residue comparison and weighted precision pass

2026-11-11. Root read the four complete reports below and verified their hashes. This supplements decision05EE39C563E731694321B98FF4049C14C8C3C640A27809B62B02E8C23044C3E7. Accept the guarded residue comparison at its conditional metadata scope and authorize the independently reviewed single-assignment weighted precision pass. No runtime saving or proof closure is asserted.

## Guarded residue comparison accepted

The exact matched baseline is fixed-profile K20/b94 in all five bands, with all five saved admissions passing. Both shorter schedules use K19/b94 in bands1-3 and K20/b94 in band4. Final-band K18/b94 or K19/b93 consumes the fresh guard-domain admission at26525/4. Source bounds remain at7000 and all twenty source slots remain produced.

For the main alternative, the baseline residue product/adder components are593964831456366888 and161959260607299648. Short-final18 has583655284972557560 and157692012712769200. Short-final19 has583652778599896288 and157692961069992384. These are the inherited arithmetic templates, not complete proof commands or seconds.

The final19 versus18 decision retains the reserve74U product events minus28U addition events, U=33869900828, against all added stage bridges, nearest-range implications, changed shifts, original-width conversions and other differential costs. Its additional shared-schedule coordinate conversions are663264800. Equal counts at different grids need not have equal costs. No winner is selected.

The reviewed full-grid conversion schedule adds41 final shared source layers while individual guard residue costs remain in their actual bands. Main coordinate-conversion totals are28734393000,27263630950 and27926895750 for baseline, final18 and final19. One admission per array remains a conditional proof-lifetime schedule. Lookups, source bindings, imports, reconstruction and transport are paid. The payload figures are numerator storage only.

Decision SHA256: DE4D83256796DA1B786F33787318126CA391D020C0EE67453AC2405F2DCA70F8


# Weighted precision gains and further error reductions

2026-11-11. The previous goal turn made progress through decisions05EE39C5 andDE4D8325. This turn accepts the independently reconstructed weighted price result, the independently reconstructed sharper assignment, and three conditional mathematical interfaces below. Root read all twelve complete reports and verified their hashes. No numerical spectral certificate, compiled declaration, full verifier implementation or end-to-end runtime saving is claimed.

## The first weighted precision gain is verified

Accept the conservative conductor-local normalized-transform price pass. Its238191 context visits reconstruct both complete125279-key old/guard histograms, then reproduce the full globalp108 comparator before forming936 local width classes. Every mode/band/component/setup field matches an independent reconstruction. Author and reviewer completed their first runs without corrections.

For the main original-plus41-guard scenario, the fixed-three arithmetic-template subtotal falls from4391036155239028824 to3955686120564196806, about9.9 percent. Product reserve is404915638126132054 and addition reserve30434396548699964. Four-product subtotal falls from5611406182071529048 to5031962625825996592. These are declared byte-template event sums, not MM0 commands, Lean reductions, machine instructions or core hours. All four auxiliary alternatives remain separate.

The conservative independent-per-p scale/sum addition subtotal rises from64162618688 to68705670272 in three-product mode. Original kernel FFT counts separately increase by151943652 complex products,383841504 complex additions and13325166 quarter turns. Those complex operations have different units from the byte-template sums and require their own precision/width-sensitive prices. Root/chirp generation, input/output embedding, signed/range proofs, identities, lifetime/replay costs and remaining work stay explicit.

Thus this is a verified component reserve. The known scale/sum increment is4543051584 in the same addition-template units, but omitted costs are not set to zero. Cold and sunk comparisons differ. Shared-high producers, sharper assignments, upward portfolios and later arithmetic-library refinements receive no implicit credit in this frozen result.

## Sharper assignments are also independently reconstructed

Root approved the sharper assignment preflight after full review and required exact writer-source clearance before execution. Both author execution and independent reconstruction succeeded without a rerun. Every legacy coefficient/row/slack/cache record was reconstructed before publishing the successor. The selected safety and peak guards remain unchanged; the sharper D field is not substituted for them.

Exactly8707 rows drop one bit. The new p100 through108 populations are108741,4890,3783,2816,2017,1729,1416,8,0. The same complete globalp108 sufficient radius is retained. These populations are not runtime weights.

The sharper full-plan closure has37522 precision states,7850 kernel keys and369180928 entries. Relative to the conservative local assignment, the independent-per-p producer schedule adds244 kernel keys and4825024 entries. Equal old/new complete keys identify possible shared objects only under actual producer, enclosure and lifetime equality. No new numerical center or guard sample was produced.

A separately reviewed successor price pass is still needed. The author is preparing a smaller changed-row calculation: validate all paired rows, then propagate only the8707 one-bit changes, remove their old contributions and insert them at the new p. This is a proposed bounded update, not yet an accepted price or execution authorization. It must preserve nonnegative exact histogram subtraction, all old/guard weights and new full-cache costs.

## Upward portfolios have a valid comparison interface

Accept the class-uniform upward map interface and root's independent review. If r is a row's least admitted p, any actual producer at g(r)>=r preserves the error and old guards. Fixed three- and four-product byte-template runtime prices are nondecreasing in p. Nearest-upward selection minimizes that restricted runtime core for a fixed allowed set, but not necessarily complete cost because cache-key unions may merge.

The newly accepted r-labelled width histogram and exact kernel membership can support a later class-uniform comparison without per-gate expansion. It does not support arbitrary within-class conductor splitting, exact global root-angle deduplication, actual source-cache lifetimes or unpriced mode switching. No portfolio enumeration or selection is authorized here. Production, proof generation and ordinary checking need separate full price functions.

## Shared unit roots have a separate two-bit projection guard

Accept the unit-root/chirp projection adapter with its independent review. A grid110 center with COMPLEX-norm error at most2^-110 from the exact required unit can be projected directly to p100 through108. Coordinate-nearest rounding contributes less than3/4*2^-p, and the two-bit source margin leaves total error below2^-p. Equal-precision reuse is a separate branch. The one-bit gap is not admitted by this triangle proof.

The projected coordinate numerator has magnitude at most2^p and retains the old p+1 magnitude-bit coefficient capacity. Raw rounding witnesses use the high numerator before any residual narrowing. Low cached sums must be exact sums of the actual projected coordinates. Ties toward positive infinity do not commute with conjugation or negation automatically; an alternative isometric recipe needs its own binding.

The exact DFT/chirp index and sign identity must be preserved. The paper does not include Gauss root numbers, canonical completion phases or transformed nonunit kernels in its shared unit catalogue. Actual identity sets and lifetimes remain unknown, so no setup reduction is credited. The previously accepted shared-high kernel adapter remains separate.

## Guaranteed Horner cancellation preserves centers

Accept the dyadic displacement adapter and independent review. With the actual ties-down anchor fixed, F=2D_out=2^f, c=2^tau*d and nu=min(tau,f), the displacement r/(2c) admits the exact reduced numerator/denominator r0/(2c0). The identities and geometric bound must be checked before using smaller product operands. The product-round tie rule remains nearest toward positive infinity and is distinct from anchor ties.

For stage delta, cancel beta=min(delta,tau+1-nu) of the remaining denominator with its alignment. The reduced signed residual witness is sound and complete for the same nearest integer. Induction preserves every accumulator and export for the SAME coefficient/grid circuit. Stored accumulator widths do not shrink merely because an intermediate rational representation changes.

Total removed denominator power is min(tau+1,nu+delta). Against a comparator already cancelling all alignment power, the extra reduction is only min(nu,max(0,tau+1-delta)). The older exact-target-reduction framework already contains this guarantee if actually proved and priced. The current guarded frontier is uncancelled. No double credit, stage deletion, residue-specific gcd shortcut or aggregate saving is accepted. Geometry setup, raw construction, shifts, signs/ranges, lookups and proof lifetimes remain costs.

## Current next research

The immediate quantitative follow-up is the reviewed changed-row price preflight. Mathematical work now targets two visibly loose error factors: final-chirp amplification bounded by3/2 and tensor amplification bounded by2. These are hypotheses for new derivations, not yet credited improvements. Another source-level investigation asks whether checked regridding below input grid100 can preserve the full error allowance with explicit input uncertainty and rederived magnitude widths. The current p>=100 results remain unchanged.

A strategic source audit is also checking whether prior weak-strip/contour work offers an actual cheaper producer for the currently selected analytic consumers. No route change is inferred from replacing an axiom's name. The objective remains minimizing full formalization work, including finite coverage, principal and small-family certificates, analytic foundations and ordinary proof generation/checking. Those parts remain open and no final Fermi estimate is issued here.
